Other specs: 05 v1.0.37 (BUG-23 Cash desk "Open" minutes), 01a v2.12 (BUG-8 Points page warnings, the owner app's remembered branch, BUG-22 hub admin PIN pad keys), 01b v1.14 (BUG-19 no autofill in the staff search and New PIN). |
| v1.0.27 | **2026-11-28, owner 2026-11-28 Q9 (QA round 1 rulings).** **(Q9) One request per bill.** "Approval A" now reads **one bill, one amount**: when several open takeaway / parking orders share one open bill (`bill_key`), ONE `RequestUnpaidHandover` covers that bill's whole due and all its orders; orders on different bills → one request per bill. §3.7a Ask / Approve, §6.4a script (the request is found by the bill: subject = the bill's oldest live order, `dedupe_key = "hand_over_unpaid:bill:" + bill_key`; any order of the bill names it), §6.12a values gain `order_ids` (every live order of the bill, oldest first; additive, optional on read) and the apply appends one `UnpaidHandoverApproved` per order of the bill at the bill's due (event unchanged), §6.16 `bill` gains `key` (the order's `bill_key` while due, `null` when paid; additive) so a client groups a token's orders by bill and never adds one bill's due twice, §8 the Pass card and the token drawer ask once per unpaid bill: **one tap** on "Ask a manager" when the token has a single unpaid bill (the card shows the waiting state), the per-bill sheet only with 2+ unpaid bills. Marker "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9)". **(Q10) Manager card:** §6.12a `details_schema` picks `disposition` too; the script sets `disposition: 'alert.hand_over_unpaid.addition'` when the request's bill holds additions only (a takeaway bill whose key `takeaway:<root>` no longer has its root live), so the card reads "Hand over unpaid #14 (addition)"; the closed card says why from the `alert_closed` frame's `note` (01d v1.3.8). §3.7a Close: a bill's request closes `handed_over` when a send hands over any order it covers (the rest of the bill asks again). Marker "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q10)". **(Q11) Order-taking screens need `take-order`.** §8: the `takeaway` and `parking` screens (tabs and their routes `#/takeaway`, `#/orders`) are shown only to a person holding `take-order` (after the 05a mask; 05a §3.6 O-16, no read-only screens); without it the tab is hidden and a link to the route shows "Not allowed". The table map (`dine_in`) is unchanged (waiters serve and clear tables without `take-order`). The default Cook (run-pass, reprint-ticket) therefore sees the table map and the Pass only; the hub already refuses `SubmitOrder` without `take-order` (§3.14). Marker "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q11)". |
| v1.0.26 | **2026-11-28, owner rulings (owner 2026-11-28; QA, Chrome 28 Nov) on top of v1.0.25 DRAFT.** CT-1 (BUG-5) the cart warns before Send when the kitchen cannot make the asked pickup; CT-2 (BUG-6) the cart's preview re-asks on the kitchen-load events the menu tiles use and every 30 s; CT-3 (BUG-7) the cart shows one clock, the customer time, on part rows, the line badge and "All ready by"; CT-4 `eta-preview` `lines[].ready_ms` (additive). Marked "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.26 (owner 2026-11-28)". Tests 41, 57 changed; 96–99 new. No event, migration, endpoint or error code. |
| v1.0.25 | **DRAFT (pending hostile review + owner), 2026-11-24 (02b-TK takeaway; owner rulings R1–R9 of 2026-11-24, `docs/plans/02b-tk-rulings.md`; backend audit of `main` at `0c71cfa`; hand-off `docs/design/handoff/02b-tk/`).** Original changes use the v1.0.25 markers. The “Hostile run 3 fixes” table records the superseding D1–D9 changes. **(1) Pickup later (R4, G-2):** §3.1, new §3.2 step 2b, step 7, §6.1, §6.14: `SubmitOrder.pickup_at_ms?` (integer ms, **takeaway only**, else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` path `pickup_at_ms`); earliest = hub now + `PICKUP_MIN_LEAD_MS` (600 000, proposed, TK-Q1); latest = end of the open business day ~~= `opened_at + day_stale_after_hours × 3 600 000` (proposed, TK-Q2; 01a has no day end)~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (01a run 2 m-13): = the first occurrence of 01a v2.11's `business_day_end_local_ms` at or after `opened_at + day_min_gap_hours × 3 600 000`, computed by 01a v2.11's `@pos/domain` helper `businessDayLatestMs(opened_at, day_min_gap_hours, business_day_end_local_ms, utc_offset_ms)` (TK-Q2 accepted; §3.2 step 2b); else **`422 PICKUP_TIME_INVALID { reason, pickup_at_ms, earliest_ms, latest_ms }`** (new, §9); a **queued** submit is checked against its accepted `queued_at` and a pickup that has passed by acceptance is accepted as given, cooked as soon as possible (proposed, TK-Q3); the value goes into `ProposedOrder.order.pickup_at_ms` (was hard-coded `null`, `build-submit.ts:58`) and so onto `OrderPlaced.pickup_at_ms` (field exists, no event change); the whole order lands at the pickup, ready / immediate lines included, which is **03a v1.2.11** (dependency, not this spec); the same field and checks in the `eta-preview` draft. A takeaway addition copies no pickup (§3.1). Name and phone **stay optional** with a pickup (R1 over G-3, TK-Q4). **(2) Phone key (R1):** §3.1, §5: the canonical key `phone_key` (strip non-digits; `0` + 10 digits → `92` + the 10; `92` + 10 stays; anything else stays as its digits; `0300 1234567`, `923001234567`, `+92 300 1234567` → `923001234567`) is computed **in the `orders` fold of `OrderContactSet`** (logged events are never rewritten); `phone` keeps the typed digits for display (unchanged). **(3) Open orders until handed over (R3, G-1):** §3.3, §6.16, §6.18 catch-up: a takeaway order stays open while it is not cancelled, not day-closed and (not billed **or** a live line is not served); parking and dine-in unchanged; rows gain `bill { state: 'due' \| 'paid', due_minor }` (05's new `keyBalances` export), `ready_at` (03b v1.0.16 `readyAtOf`, as fixed by 03b run 1: the latest Ready of any alert state; per-line `LINE_READY_AT_SQL` only in `takeawayProgress`), `handed { n, m }`, `lines_summary` (≤ 6) + `lines_more`, `pickup_at_ms`; **no `customer_name`, no phone digits** (R3: search runs on the kiosk from the catch-up); | v1.0.13 | Owner, 2026-11-12 (04 Q-19 (b), 21:03 PKT; 04 #42 / T1 PR #138 STOP Q-1). **Null-quantity option child cascade (01c v1.1.2 §3.4.8, decision Q4, test 126).** An `option_child` added to a submitted line later has `ItemAdded.option_quantity: null`. §3.4 cascade rule for `cascadeUnits` and so for `planVoid`, `planRemove` and the comp cascades (`planComp`: `CompLine`, `applyComp`): a **partial** parent void / remove / comp (n < the parent's units left for that action) gives such a child **0** units (it gets no event and is not a touched id); the parent's **last** unit (for comp its last uncomped live unit), or a whole-line action, gives it **every remaining unit, capped** (`min(cap, child units left)`, cap = live for remove / void, live − comped for comp). The null rule is tied to the line kind: it applies **only** to an `option_child` (its parent's `line_kind` is `'item'`) whose `option_quantity` is null. The child-units-are-a-multiple-of-the-parent's check and the ratio cascade apply to every other child: placed option children (`option_quantity` set) and **every deal part**, which is always written with `option_quantity: null` (`build-submit.ts:186`; 01c Q4 "null on every other kind") and always keeps its per-portion cascade (01c N1, :309). A null option child never throws and is never cascaded by ratio, even when its units happen to be a multiple. `LedgerRef` gains `option_quantity: number \| null`. §5: `order_lines` has no `option_quantity` today (v1.0.12 DDL; `return-to-kitchen.ts:70`), so `order_lines` gains `option_quantity INTEGER` (the line's `ItemAdded.option_quantity`; `NULL` on every non-`option_child` line and on a later-added child), written by the `orders` fold of `ItemAdded`; hub migration **`0020_order_line_option_quantity.sql`** (next free after v1.0.12's `0019_order_line_station.sql`) adds it and deletes the `orders` checkpoint, so `orders` refolds from seq 0 (00 v1.10 H-2, as 0019). §4.2: `readOrder`'s line row gains `option_quantity` (`l.option_quantity`, the row it already reads: still **1** statement); `CompLine` / `applyComp` read it in their existing line read (still 1). No statement count changes. Test 39 gains the null-child cases. Today no path writes a null child (`build-submit.ts:224` always sets it); the ledger is made right ahead of 02's later additions. Marked "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.13 (owner 2026-11-12, 04 Q-19)". No event, command or error change. **Z3:** the `module-04` → `main` merge writes **v1.0.14** = main's v1.0.11 + v1.0.12 + v1.0.13 (tests 39 extension, 41–51; README keeps v1.0.11, v1.0.12, v1.0.13). |

## Owner rulings 2026-11-28 QA-R2 (QA round 2, kiosk / tablet) — v1.0.28

⚠️ Changed by v1.0.28 (owner 2026-11-28 QA-R2). Client only (`apps/client` staff entry, `packages/ui-kit`); the hub, its wire and its events are unchanged. Each row is binding for §8.

| Row | Owner ruling (2026-11-28 QA-R2) | Change | Where | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| QK-1 | BUG-5 (MAJOR): "Never show masked data to a user who may see it; if the data comes back masked, refetch; if the session ended, say 'You were signed out'." | **Cause found:** each mount of the takeaway screen made a new token book seeded from the live bus's **last catch-up** (taken when the socket connected), so every `OrderContactSet` heard after it was lost when the person left Takeaway (My shift, Cash desk, Pass…) and came back; a reload got a fresh catch-up. Now: (a) the token book is the **page's**, one per live bus, fed by every frame while no screen is mounted, and **emptied when the session ends** (the bus's `forget`, BUG-12), so nothing of one person's carries to the next; (b) **contact gap → re-read:** an `open-orders` row whose contact the hub sends this socket (O17: every order on a `cashier_kiosk`, the person's own orders elsewhere) but the book lacks — the book never heard of the order, or the row `has_phone` and no phone is known — for 2 s → the page reopens its socket in the same mode (a fresh catch-up, `AlertConnection.resync`); the same orders are asked once per screen (no loop against a hub that does not send them); (c) a session the hub ended answers the reopen with 4401 → the existing sign-in screen line "You were signed out. Sign in again." (BUG-12). The book still never asks the hub for contact (R3; `order-contact` stays the logged Call path). | §8 Takeaway queue rail, Token view | QR2-1, QR2-2 |
| QK-2 | BUG-15: "Kiosk at 1366–1456 px: tiles ~80 px with names broken per letter, pickup chips '+30 m…', 'Print check bill' clipped, qty badge clipped. Layout must work from 1366 up (and 1280 tablet)." | From **1366 px** wide (kiosk) the menu keeps tiles **≥ 140 px** wide (fewer columns, never narrower tiles), names wrap **by word** (never per letter), the pickup chips, the cart foot buttons (Print check bill, Send) and the quantity badge are never clipped (they wrap or shrink their padding). The 1280 × 800 landscape tablet keeps its layout (strip + 3 columns + 440 px cart). | §8 Takeaway home (kiosk), `staff-02b-tk.css` | QR2-3 (e2e, kiosk at 1366 × 768) |
| QK-3 | BUG-16: "Urdu: category chips and 'Sign in to see details' still English (if chips come from menu data, use Urdu names when present and report the data gap); pickup chips truncated. Add a language switch in the person menu (EN / اردو), remembered per device." | The lock text has its Urdu string. Category chips show the menu's names as the owner typed them: the menu data has **no Urdu name field** today (01a/01c), so there is nothing to show — **data gap reported**, no schema change here. The pickup chips fit in Urdu (QK-2 rules). The person menu gains **Language: English / اردو**; the choice is stored on the device (`pos-lang`, as S49's `?lang=`) and applies at once without signing out (the page re-renders in the new language; the session, socket and the cart's draft stay). | §8 Shell (person menu), i18n | QR2-4 |
| QK-4 | BUG-17: "A past pickup time says 'That time has passed' (not 'Too late — the day ends at 4:00 AM'), and the cart keeps its times." | Time…: a typed time earlier than hub now is **today's** time when tomorrow's would fall after the open business day's end (`pickup_bounds.latest_ms`), so it is not sent as tomorrow; a cart pickup (chip or time) at or before hub now shows **"That time has passed"** with the range, and Send stays disabled until a new pickup is chosen (the hub's PICKUP_TIME_INVALID texts stay for its other reasons). The cart keeps its pickup and keeps showing its last previewed ready times while the pickup is refused (never "—" for that reason). | §8 Phone and pickup | QR2-5 |
| QK-5 | BUG-21: "'/' must focus item search from anywhere on the order screen, including right after clicking a top tab." | "/" focuses the menu search when focus is on any non-editable element of the page (a top-bar tab, a rail row, the page itself) and the token drawer may be open; never from a text field, a dialog, a menu or the lock. Other typed letters keep their v1.0.25 rule (only on the screen itself). | §8 Keyboard speed mode | QR2-6 |
| QK-6 | BUG-23 (rail): "offline rail header 'COOKING 0' with 4 listed." | The queue's headers count what they list: the tablet strip's "All orders · n" and "n Cooking" include the takeaways waiting to send in the device queue (as the rail's Cooking section already does). Tile states stay as they are (lane r2-pass). | §8 Takeaway queue rail | QR2-7 |
| QK-7 | BUG-24: "Mixed Urdu/English/emoji names render in wrong bidi order in Held (dir='auto' / isolates); name input shows a 40-char counter; 999n says 'Max 99'." | Every customer name shown on the order screen (Held, rail, drawer, context bar) is an isolated run (`<bdi>` / `dir="auto"`), so a mixed name keeps its own order inside the line. The cart's name field shows **"n/40"** (the submit limit, §6.1 `customer_name` ≤ 40) and does not take more. Keyboard mode: a quantity above 99 ("999n") adds 99 and the search shows **"Max 99"**. | §8 Held, Customer row, Keyboard speed mode | QR2-8 |
| QK-8 | BUG-26: "After sign-in, max 1 summary toast ('3 running late · 6 new') — no stack over the menu." | The alert banners raised **before** this sign-in are not stacked over the screen: one summary toast **"{n} running late · {m} new"** (either part left out at 0; tap → Inbox) replaces them for this sign-in; alerts raised after it show as banners as before (01d §8.1 B01 unchanged). "Running late" = the late kinds (kind names containing `late`). | §8 Shell, 01d banners (client) | QR2-9 |
| QK-9 | UX: "After Open shift land on the screen you came from (Takeaway), not My shift." | A shift opened from a prompt on another screen (e.g. Takeaway's "Open your shift") returns to that screen; opened from My shift it stays there. | §8 Shell | QR2-10 |
| QK-10 | UX: "Double-click on panel buttons doesn't select text." | Buttons, chips, tiles and rail rows of the staff page are `user-select: none` (text fields and readable text stay selectable). | §8 (ui-kit) | QR2-11 |
| QK-11 | — | Tests QR2-1 – QR2-11 are client tests (vitest; QR2-3 Playwright). | §10 | — |
| QK-12 | — | No event, migration, endpoint, wire field or error code; no other module's contract. | — | — |

## §1. Header

### 1.1 Stories covered

E2 "Table management and order types" AC9 (dine-in, takeaway, parking; delivery and web deferred); E2 "Adding items to a running order" AC1–AC4; E2 "Per-item ETA preview" AC1–AC6; E2 "Sold out and slow items" AC1–AC3; E0 "Hold/park, notes, reorder, reprint" AC1 (device only, manager view deferred) and AC2; E0 "Refunds, cancellations, complimentary items" AC3, AC4 (stage 1), AC5; E5 "Device resilience during hub restart" AC1–AC4 and its "order in progress preserved" edge case; 01d-g0 Z3–Z5, Z7; C2–C3; G0-6 (Served by tap); owner (02-g0 §5.4, 02b-g0 §5.4) Q4 token, Q5, Q6, Q7, Q8, and 02b Q1–Q6.

### 1.2 In scope

Orders and lines (`SubmitOrder`, rounds, `follow_up`, ids), the `ItemAdded` copy and `ProposedOrder`, availability / windows / stock / pause / option checks, zone routing at submit, zone-served lines, stage-1 removal (whole or partial) and `OrderCancelled`, the per-line money ledger, weighed lines, Served by tap (estimated Served specified, registered with 03b), `ReleaseItems`, `BoostOrder`, `ReturnToKitchen`, comps (command, event, approval kind), zone stock (counts, refill as a transfer, set count, low / out alerts), takeaway token, parking fields, order notes, `seatingTotals`, the device command queue (hub acceptance rules and the client queue), the ordering screen, the orders part of the table view, open orders (incl. abandoned), held orders (device-local). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK audit, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): also the takeaway pickup time (`pickup_at_ms`, R4), the phone key and the phone lookup with repeat (`customer-orders`, R1), open takeaway orders until handed over (R3), `top-items` (R9) and the §8 client notes of the 02b-TK hand-off.

### 1.3 Out of scope

Delivery and web order types (P2 / P4, §12.4); later child additions to a submitted parent (§12.4); ~~reorder (§12.4)~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): reorder by phone is in scope, §6.18b); editing a kitchen note after submit (§12.4); sibling-zone transfer (Z6), day-end zone recount prompt (Z8), nested zones (§12.4); tables, seatings, lock, handover, move / merge / split, bill request, cleaning (**02a**); engine math and its events' engine fields (**03a**); Start / Done, Ready to serve, Handover, KDS, tickets, the Served card (**03b**); voids at stage 2+, move-to-stock, remake billing, the customer's own wrong order (**04**); bills, payments, discounts, the comp's bill effect, Z report (**05**); hub-down manager alert (**06 / 01e**); ~~customer tracking (P2)~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R5, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): customer status page in the pilot, **00 Hub Platform Access v1.13**, §12.4); the pickup board (TV) and its display session (R7, 00 / 05a, later 02b bump).

### 1.4 Decisions log (developer, 2026-11-07)

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| O1 | **A round is a new order.** `OrderLinesAdded` only for `ReturnToKitchen` (D2, D7; 02b-g0 §3 #2). ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling 2026-11-09 (E-4, 02b v1.0.2): this is 02b's choice under 03a v1.2 To 02 #1 — a later round ("drinks first, food later", one more naan after the meal) is always a new `OrderPlaced` (with `follow_up`), never an `OrderLinesAdded`; the engine would accept either (03a test 139). Every `OrderLinesAdded` 02b writes carries `order_header` (§3.8). |
| O2 | **One line with quantity** (01c D-2); partial actions name `units`. |
| O3 | **Ids from the device** (03a AD-29 Q5): `order_id` and every `order_item_id` are ULIDs minted when the line enters the cart and kept for every retry. |
| O4 | **Zone routing resolved by 02b** (Z3): a dine-in line's starting station is the route of the seating's primary table's zone, else the branch route. Zones are flat (01a v2.2); nesting deferred. |
| O5 | **Zone-served lines** (Z4): a plain item line punched at a table whose zone holds it is taken from the zone count, not an engine line, served in the submit batch with `source: 'zone'`, never printed, never counted as "eating". |
| O6 | **Refill is a transfer** (owner Q1): a refill moves the item's day stock draws out of branch stock; a count never touches branch stock. |
| O7 | **Partial remove and partial comp** (owner Q2, Q3): both take `units`, through one per-line money ledger (§3.4). |
| O8 | **No day, no order** (owner Q4). |
| O9 | **Lock scope** (D39 extended): only `SubmitOrder` and `RemoveItems` call `requireLock`; 02a row P-1e records it. |
| O10 | **Non-table orders belong to the person who placed them** for `own_tables` and `my_floors` (owner Q6, B-4). |
| O11 | **Queued auto-send** (owner Q8, 00 H-1, simplified 2026-11-07): only queueable command types; the session stays alive while its device is disconnected (idle clock paused, ≤ 4 h), so the queue replays as normal live commands. A command whose session has ended is never accepted; it goes to the queue log (§3.12). |
| O12 | **Token** for takeaway only; from 1 per business date per branch. |
| O13 | **Comps on submitted lines only**; comps and every PIN-capable action are online only. |
| O14 | **Estimated Served** is specified here and registered when 03b's `Handover` exists (no tick before that). ⚠️ Changed by 03b v1.0.1 (G-2): the tick is written by 03b task 0e over `handoversDue`, building its drafts with the extracted `servedDrafts(…, 'estimated')`. |
| O15 | **Short stock** is refused (03a AD-19); the client caps the stepper. |
| O16 | **Served needs a fired line** (stages: fired-not-started, cooking, cooked); unfired lines are never served (F-1). A line of an order that has **left the engine** (abandon or exit) counts as fired for Served and as past stage 1 for Remove. **Remove** = stage 1 = engine stage `added` (fired or not, §3.5, F §4.2); a fired line's removal prints a CANCELLED slip (03b). |
| O17 | **Customer contact** (phone, name) is never on `OrderPlaced`: it travels in its own event `OrderContactSet`, whose audience is cashier kiosks and the placer's sessions; others see `has_phone` and fetch it on demand (`order-contact`, logged). It stays in the immutable event log and the cloud (§12.3 Q1 for the owner). |
| O18 | **One late card per current table** (owner, 2026-11-07; simplified the same day): the engine and 03a's `fires_alerts` tick stay as built — one `slip_warning` per original seating (02a T21, `table_key` never re-keyed). The **screen** groups the open `slip_warning`s whose seating now belongs to the same current table (02a `currentTableOf` on the alert's oldest order, `values.order_ids`; the table never comes from `table_key`, 02a :251) into **one card** ("T4 + T5 — 2 late"); each warning keeps its own buttons inside the card. A joined table may ring twice; it shows once. After a split each warning shows under its oldest order's current table. No engine or tick change (03a row Q-7 dropped). |
| O19 | **Deal money follows 01c §3.4.8** (run 2 #1): a `deal_part` line carries its own refundable amount and ledger; the deal line's billed amount is its total minus its parts' money out. |
| O20 | **Remade units stay billable** (run 2 #2): a remake frees nothing by itself; the manager comps or 04 voids the burnt dish if it should be free. Only the replacement line is free. |
| O21 | **A voided comp stays a comp** (run 2 #15): voiding a comped unit moves its share to `comp_voided_minor`, reported by 05 as a voided comp, never as a refund. |

---

## §2. Existing architecture that must not change

- 00: `POST /commands/:name`, device + staff session, D32 idempotency (`processed_commands`), single writer `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, commit-then-swap (D45), projections with `reset`, ticks, catch-up, audiences, `StaffDirectory`, `clock`, `ids`, M19 replay fast path, pipeline order 00 §3.7. The envelope has no free-form field (F §4.1, `packages/events/src/envelope.ts`).
- Foundation §5.9 submit pipeline; §4.2 stages and guard; §4.3 lock (D39, D51, D61); §4.1 stream types (`order, table, station, stock, shift, audit, hub, staff`).
- 01a: `branchConfig()` (snapshot V4), `openDayStartMs(state)`, `BusinessDayOpened` / `BusinessDayClosed`, `BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, stock events, `item_availability`, `hub_time`.
- 01b: `resolve(staff, permission)`, `PERMISSION_SCOPES`, scopes `branch`, `my_floors`, `own_tables`, B-2 zone assignment (one order taker per zone), `reports_to`; C8.
- 01c: `ItemAdded` v1 (§4.3), `menu-lines.ts` (`orderLineTotalMinor`, `allocateDealMinor`, `checkOptionChoices`, `isGroupSatisfiable`, `isItemOrderable`, `childQuantity`, `optionChargeMinor`), engine stock helpers.
- 01d: `defineAlertKind`, `alerts.raise` / `raiseMany` / `close` / `closeMany` / `byDedupeKey` / `listOpen`, `registerTick`, `APPROVAL_TIER_STEPS`, read-only request `apply`, PIN commands never queued, target kinds §3.4.
- 02a: `seatingOf`, `currentTableOf`, `requireLock`, `zoneOf`, `holdersOfOrders`, projection `tables` (writes `seatings`, `seating_tables`, `seating_orders`, `table_locks`).
- 03a (as built on `module-03a`): `ProposedOrder`, `checkOrderable`, `reserveStock`, `planOrder(state, proposed, now, from_stock)`, `calculateETA(state, proposed, now, from_stock?)`, `stockEffects`, `planRelease`, `isOrderInEngine`, the `order` events' engine fields, `stations-live`, `slip_warning`.

---

## §3. Domain rules

### 3.1 Order types and identity

| Type | Identity | `table_key` | Lock | Extra fields |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dine_in` | the seating (02a) | `'dine_in:' + seating_id` (02a T21; never re-keyed, O18) | `requireLock` | — |
| `takeaway` | token | `'takeaway:' + order_id` | none | `token`, `customer_name?`, `phone?` |
| `parking` | car number | `'parking:' + car_number_norm` | none | `car_number`, `car_type`, `phone?` |

`car_number_norm` = upper-case, spaces and dashes removed; `car_number` stored as typed (2–12 of letters, digits, space, dash). `car_type` ∈ `sedan | mini | large` (required, S10). `phone`: 10–13 digits, stored digits only. `customer_name` ≤ 40. Two parking orders with the same plate share a `table_key` (the same car). `OrderPlaced.table_id` = the seating's primary table at submit (03a field); 02b keeps no copy of it.

**Phone key** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT)): phone **stays optional** on takeaway and parking. The typed phone is validated and stored as today (`^\+?[\d\s-]+$`, 10–13 digits, `phone` = the typed digits only, shown as typed; `submit-order.ts:27-33`, unchanged). For **matching** (§6.18b) every phone has one canonical key, `phone_key = phoneKey(phone)`, a pure function of the stored digits `d`:

1. `d` = the digits only (already so in the event);
2. `d` is 11 digits starting with `0` (`0` + 10) → `'92' + d.slice(1)`;
3. `d` is 12 digits starting with `92` (`92` + 10, also a typed `+92 …`) → `d`;
3a. (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-2, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) `d` is 13 digits starting with `920` (`+92 0300 …`, a common mixed form) → `'92' + d.slice(3)`;
3b. (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-2, TK-Q5 default, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) `d` is 10 digits starting with `3` (a mobile typed without its `0`) → `'92' + d`;
4. anything else → `d` unchanged.

(⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-2, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) The international `00` prefix (`0092 300 1234567`, 14 digits) is **refused** by the unchanged 10–13-digit rule, at `SubmitOrder` and at `customer-orders`; the client strips a leading `00` before sending (its phone mask, §8). The client sends the query value with `encodeURIComponent` (an unencoded `+` decodes to a space, harmless after stripping). Any later change to `phoneKey` is a **migration with an `orders` checkpoint delete** (refold), never a silent code change.

| Typed | `phone` (stored, shown) | `phone_key` |
|---|---|---|
| `0300 1234567` | `03001234567` | `923001234567` |
| `0300-1234567` | `03001234567` | `923001234567` |
| `923001234567` | `923001234567` | `923001234567` |
| `+92 300 1234567` | `923001234567` | `923001234567` |
| `042 35761234` (landline, `0` + 10) | `04235761234` | `924235761234` |
| `3001234567` (10 digits, no leading `0`) | `3001234567` | `923001234567` (rule 3b, run 1 m-2; was `3001234567`) |
| `+92 0300 1234567` | `9203001234567` | `923001234567` (rule 3a, run 1 m-2) |
| `+44 20 7946 0958` | `442079460958` | `442079460958` (rule 4) |

Logged `OrderContactSet` events are never rewritten: the key is computed **in the `orders` projection's fold of `OrderContactSet`** (column `orders.phone_key`, §5; `NULL` when `phone` is null), so it holds for every event ever logged after the `NNNN` refold. `phoneKey` lives with the fold (`apps/hub/src/orders/phone-key.ts`, pure, module-internal, §4.2); `phone_key` never leaves the hub (no query body, frame or catch-up carries it).

**Pickup time** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT)): a takeaway may carry `pickup_at_ms` (the customer collects later; phone-ahead order). Rules in §3.2 step 2b. It is **takeaway only**; it is copied to `ProposedOrder.order.pickup_at_ms` and `OrderPlaced.pickup_at_ms` (03a field, already in the schema) and folded into `orders.pickup_at_ms` (§5). **Name and phone stay optional with a pickup** (R1; the hand-off's G-3 "phone required when the pickup is later" is overruled by R1, R4 says nothing that implies it; the client may prompt for them, the hub never requires them; TK-Q4). A **takeaway addition** (`adds_to_order_id`) copies nothing from its root, the pickup included (as the contact, R-34): it carries its own `pickup_at_ms` or none; the client pre-fills the root's pickup ~~while it is still in the future~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-8, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): only while the root's pickup ≥ hub now + `PICKUP_MIN_LEAD_MS`, else none, so the pre-fill never fails `too_early`) (§8). 03a To 02 #15 (d) ("an addition keeps the order's pickup") is about `OrderLinesAdded` (returns), which copy `order_header.pickup_at_ms` from the `EngineOrder` (§3.8); a takeaway addition is a new `OrderPlaced` and follows this rule.

**Non-engine lines of a pickup order** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-13; 03a v1.2.11 To 02 #15 (b), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)): the engine holds every engine line for the pickup (03a PK-1); a line that is **no engine line** (no station: a packed item or a ready item with no station; zone lines never serve takeaway) is 02b's to hold: it is handed **with the order** — the client's "Give ready items now" never offers it before `pickup_at_ms`, and its `ready_at` (§4.2, §6.16) is `null` while effective pickup (§3.8) is in the future. A boost ends the time hold immediately. Counter and Pass apply whole-order readiness and §6.4’s customer hand-over path. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4)

⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): a **takeaway addition** (`SubmitOrder` for `takeaway` with `adds_to_order_id`, §3.2 step 2) is a new order of its own: it gets **its own `table_key`** (`'takeaway:' + order_id`, so 03a slip grouping is unchanged) and carries **only its own payload's customer fields** (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6, R-34): was "and copies the customer fields"; its `takeaway.customer_name` / `phone`, when given, go in its own `OrderContactSet` as for any order (O17); **nothing is copied from the root**: the root keeps its contact, and 05 groups the bill by the root's `bill_key`, not by contact), and it **keeps the root order's token** (§3.11). New column **`orders.bill_key`** (§5) for **takeaway / parking only**, `NULL` for dine-in (05 bills dine-in through `seating_orders`): takeaway `'takeaway:' + root` (root = the `adds_to_order_id` order's own root, else the order itself), parking `table_key || ':' || business_date` (the normalised plate); folded from `OrderPlaced` only. No lock (takeaway has none, table above).

### 3.2 Submit (`SubmitOrder`)

1. **Day:** `openDayStartMs(state)` null → `409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN` (O8). `business_date` from the open day.
2. **Type:** `dine_in` needs `{ table_id, seating_id }` (both required); `seatingOf(table_id)` ≠ given → `409 TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED`; `requireLock(tx, { table_id, seating_id }, { ...ctx, queued })` → its drafts (02a; P-1b: a queued submit never takes a free lock); **`queued` = the request carried a valid `X-Queued-At` on a queueable command (§3.12)**. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-1, owner 2026-11-11): `{ ...ctx, queued }` is 02a v1.1.3's `ctx.queuedAt !== null`; a queued command never takes or moves a lock (02a v1.1.3) (was: no 02a version cited). ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): `takeaway` accepts the optional **`adds_to_order_id`**: a takeaway order of the **same business date**, not cancelled; unknown → `404 ORDER_NOT_FOUND`; another type, another date or a cancelled order → `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'adds_to_order_id' }` (an earlier-day order is never a target). `OrderPlaced` carries `adds_to_order_id` (03a v1.2.6, 05 row S-1). The new order keeps the root order's token (step 9, §3.11).
2b. **Pickup** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-3, m-7, m-14, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)): `pickup_at_ms` absent or `null` → no pickup (today's behaviour). Present on a `dine_in` or `parking` payload → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { issues: [{ path: 'pickup_at_ms', message: 'takeaway_only' }] }` (Foundation §6.1 shape; was "reason `takeaway_only`"); not an integer → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (Zod). For a takeaway, with `now` = hub time at the check and `day` = the open day from step 1 (in memory, `EngineState.business_day`):
   - **earliest** = `t0 + PICKUP_MIN_LEAD_MS`, `PICKUP_MIN_LEAD_MS = 600 000` (10 min; a constant in `@pos/domain`, not a setting; proposed, TK-Q1), where `t0` = `now`, or the accepted (clamped) `queued_at` when the command was queued (§3.12);
   - **latest** = the **end of the open business day** (TK-Q2, accepted). 01a had no day end (S33: the day is closed by an action); ~~v2.11 adds none~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (integration, 02b-TK): 01a v2.11 rows BD-1 – BD-3 add it (branch column, 01b v1.12 settings wire, optional top-level snapshot key; absent → `BUSINESS_DAY_END_LOCAL_MS_DEFAULT` 14 400 000). The 01a branch setting **`business_day_end_local_ms`** (hub-local clock time, ~~0–86 399 999~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (01a run 2): whole minutes 0–86 340 000 (a multiple of 60 000), default **14 400 000 = 04:00**; row TK-8); `latest` = the first instant at that local time (`hubTime()` offset, S47) that is ≥ `day.opened_at + day_min_gap_hours × 3 600 000` — ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (01a run 2 m-13): computed by 01a v2.11's `@pos/domain` helper `businessDayLatestMs(opened_at, day_min_gap_hours, business_day_end_local_ms, utc_offset_ms)` (positive modulo; 01a test 218), never re-derived in 02b. So a day opened 08:00 ends 04:00 next morning (a 00:30 pickup phoned at 22:00 is allowed), a day opened at 03:00 ends at 04:00 the next morning, not an hour later; it never depends on `day_stale_after_hours`, which keeps its one meaning (the forgotten-close prompt). Was (v1.0.25 first draft): `day.opened_at + day_stale_after_hours × 3 600 000`, which on a stale day put `latest` in the past and refused every pickup (run 1 M-3); ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1)
   - refusals, in this order, each `422 PICKUP_TIME_INVALID { reason, pickup_at_ms, earliest_ms, latest_ms }`, nothing appended: `earliest_ms > latest_ms` (no pickup time left in this day) → `reason: 'no_time_left'` for any pickup (the client offers "Now" or "Close the day first", §8); `pickup_at_ms < day.opened_at` → `reason: 'other_day'` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-7, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): a pickup typed offline for an earlier business day goes to the queue log for a person, never cooked the next morning); `pickup_at_ms < earliest` → `reason: 'too_early'`; `pickup_at_ms > latest` → `reason: 'after_day_end'`; both bounds inclusive;
   - **queued submit that arrives after its pickup** (TK-Q3, accepted; carry into 03a PK-9 / Q-PK-3): checked against `queued_at` as above, so a phone order typed offline at 1:00 PM for 1:30 PM and replayed at 1:40 PM is **accepted with the pickup as given**; the engine retains the elapsed pickup and schedules as soon as feasible (03a v1.2.11 PK-1, PK-9; row 02b-R4-2), and `OrderPlaced.pickup_at_ms` and `orders.pickup_at_ms` keep the value as given (history), and the result's `promise_ms` shows the real time ("time updated", step 11). Refusing it would drop a phone order into the queue log while the customer is on the way; clamping would rewrite what the cashier promised. A pickup later than the end of the day open **at acceptance**, or before that day's open, is still refused. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1)
   0 statements (the day from `EngineState`, the settings from `branchConfig()`).
   - **`takeaway.print_slip`** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c); 03c v1.0.16 rows Q-TK-1, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)): `takeaway.print_slip?: boolean` (optional, default `false`; a key inside `takeaway`, so takeaway only, since `takeaway` on another type is already refused); a non-boolean → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` path `takeaway.print_slip`. Honoured on a root **and** on an addition; 03c's addition rule decides (`slip_held`). The slip itself is step 10a.
3. **Ids** (O3): ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (integration, 02b-TK) (00 HPA v1.13 M-3, owner decision OD-10 = TK-Q17): `order_id` must match `ULID_RE` (`packages/domain/src/ids.ts`, 26 Crockford base-32 characters) — else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { issues: [{ path: 'order_id', message: 'not_ulid' }] }` (Foundation §6.1 shape), checked in memory before any statement, nothing appended; 00 HPA's `statusLink` gives `null` for a non-ULID id, so every order must carry one (the client already mints ULIDs, O3, R14). An id repeated inside the payload → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (path `lines.N.order_item_id`, C6); an id already in `orders` / `order_lines` or live in `EngineState` → `409 ORDER_ITEM_ID_IN_USE { order_item_id }` or `{ order_id }`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.20 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-5 phase 1): the ids checked also include `<id>-p2` … `<id>-p<n>` for every line (and linked option) of quantity n > 1 (a superset of the step 4a part ids; same statement).
4. **Lines from config** (D25): item / variant / deal from `branchConfig()`; missing → `404 MENU_ITEM_NOT_FOUND` / `DEAL_NOT_FOUND`; manual Off → `409 ITEM_UNAVAILABLE`; outside its or its category's windows on hub time → `409 ITEM_OUT_OF_HOURS { item_id, opens_at_ms | null }` (S47) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-19, owner 2026-11-11): a deal outside its windows → `409 ITEM_OUT_OF_HOURS { deal_id, opens_at_ms }` (§9) (was: only `{ item_id, … }`)); options through `checkOptionChoices` → `422 OPTION_CHOICE_INVALID { order_item_id, violations }`; a required group not satisfiable → `ITEM_UNAVAILABLE`.
4a. **Split per unit** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.20 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-5 phase 1); 03a AD-4 fairness): a cart line with `line_kind 'item'`, quantity n > 1, the day sale unit `piece`, not `requires_weighing`, not `from_zone`, whose routed station (step 6) is a **`slot`** station, is built as **n lines of quantity 1**: part 1 keeps the cart's `order_item_id`, part k (2 … n) is `<id>-p<k>`; each linked option of the line gives one child per part (`<child id>` for part 1, `<child id>-p<k>` for part k, `option_quantity` unchanged, child quantity by `childQuantity` per part); plain options, the kitchen note and every copied field go to every part. Every part carries **`part_of`** = part 1's `order_item_id`; every child carries `part_of` = part 1's child id. Prices, stock draws and `line_total_minor` follow from the per-part quantity by the existing rules (the parts' sum equals the unsplit line, integer minor units). Batch-station, weighed (`g` / `ml`), zone and deal lines are one line as before; a line of quantity 1 has no `part_of`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.23 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-6) (03a v1.2.8 BQ-6): the routed station may be a **`slot` or `batch`** station: a piece line on a batch station (tandoor, fryer; not weighed, not zone) is split per unit the same way, so 03a puts its first parts into the free spots of the earliest joinable load and the rest into the next load(s) ("×10 on an 8-load holding 5 → 3 now, 7 next"); weighed, zone and quantity-1 lines stay one line. Was: "Batch-station, weighed (`g` / `ml`), zone and deal lines are one line as before". ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.22 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-5 phase 5): a **deal** line of quantity n > 1 with a chosen part on a slot station (a `piece`, not weighed, item) is split per deal unit (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.23 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-6): a chosen part on a slot **or batch** station; was: "on a slot station") — n deal lines of quantity 1, header `<id>-p<k>`, each choice and its linked options `<…>-p<k>`, `part_of` = part 1's header (headers) or part 1's choice id (parts); each deal priced as one deal.
4b. **Max delay** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.24 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-6, STAB-7); 03a v1.2.8 §3.5.7 PR-1, To 02 #13): every engine line built here (each kind except a `deal` header, each part of step 4a) gets `max_delay_ms` from its own item in `branchConfig()` (01a v2.7.4 MD-5): `max_delay_unlimited` → `null`; else the item's `max_delay_ms` when not null; else the branch default `engine_settings.max_delay_default_ms`: *post-review 2026-11-23 (M-8)* an explicit `null` is copied as `null` (no limit), and **only a snapshot without the key** gives 300 000 (`'max_delay_default_ms' in engine_settings ? engine_settings.max_delay_default_ms : 300000`; never `?? 300000`, which turns "No limit" into 5 min). Was (2026-11-22): "`engine_settings.max_delay_default_ms ?? 300000` (which may be `null` = no limit)". 02b copies `null` as is; the engine applies its 30-min far cap (03a v1.2.8 PR-1). A `deal` header gets `null`; a `deal_part` and an `option_child` use their own item. Written on the line's `ItemAdded` (step 10) and nowhere else (no column). The same rule applies to the lines of an addition (`OrderLinesAdded`); a return line (§3.8) copies the served line's value.
5. **Zone-served** (O5, §3.10): a line marked `from_zone: true`, `line_kind 'item'`, no options, `dine_in`, the primary table's zone lists the item, and `on_hand − claimed ≥ quantity` (claims counted across the payload in line order, as `reserveStock` does) → zone-served, `served_from_zone_id` set. Any check failing → the line is a **normal kitchen line** (routed per step 6, `zone_served: false` in the result) — never a refusal, so a queued order with a zone item is not lost when the zone emptied meanwhile (run 4 M-4). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-8, owner 2026-11-10): the pure builder (§4.2 `buildSubmit`) builds every zone candidate as a **kitchen line** in `proposed` and `itemAdded` and lists its id in `zoneCandidates`; `SubmitOrder` then removes the zone-served ones from `proposed` after the zone count check and sets `served_from_zone_id` on their `ItemAdded` (was: no order of these steps stated).
6. **Routing** (O4): each engine line's `station_id` = the zone route of the primary table's zone (dine-in only) else the branch route; `alternates`, points and carry from 01a A-9 / A-10 for the order type. ⚠️ Changed by 03b v1.0.16 owner clarification (PT-R1): each engine line's `point_id` = `routePoint(config.points, station, order.order_type).point_id` (03b §4.4), not `station.point_id` (`null` only for a line with no station). The line's `carry_ms` and every alternate's `carry_ms` read the station's `carry_times` row for that routed point (missing = 0). On the `OrderLinesAdded` path (today only `ReturnToKitchen`, O1) the line copies the served line’s `point_id` if active (see §3.8 for an archived point); any new-line builder there calls `routePoint` with the order's type. The point stays fixed across ordinary routing edits. On effective archive, route outstanding and returned lines by order type against the incoming active points, most specific then effective fallback, in the same writer transaction as `BranchConfigApplied`. 03b’s registered re-route event records destinations for replay. If any line has no active destination, refuse effective archive with `VALIDATION_FAILED { issues: [{ path: "archived", message: "Activate another Pass point first" }] }` and commit no moves. 01a increments the optimistic-lock version on archive or routing edit; connected Pass screens refetch after commit and the receiving Pass shows “Moved from <old point>” (D7; 01a §6.9a). 0 statements. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-3)
7. **Build** `ProposedOrder` (03a §4.2): `lines` = every line except zone-served ones (deal headers included; the engine skips them, 03a :245); `order: { order_type, table_id, table_key, follow_up, pickup_at_ms: null }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): `pickup_at_ms` = the takeaway's checked `pickup_at_ms` (step 2b), else `null`; `buildSubmit` reads it from `input`, `build-submit.ts:58` today hard-codes `null`; was: always `null`). `OrderPlaced.order_item_ids` = the same ids in the same order (03a `OrderPlaced` field; `ProposedOrder` has none). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-8, AD-12, AD-16, owner 2026-11-10 / 2026-11-11): steps 4–7 are the pure `buildSubmit(config, input, ctx)` (§4.2); `ctx` = `{ now, utc_offset_ms, isAvailable, draws, primary_table_id | null, follow_up }`, read by the caller: `now` and `utc_offset_ms` from the hub's `hubTime()` (windows on hub local time, S47; never the runtime time zone), `isAvailable` = the `item_availability` callback (step 4), `draws` = the day-frozen sale unit and stock draws of an item (`dayDraws`, 01c V20), copied into `ItemAdded.stock_draws` and the proposed line, `primary_table_id` from 02a `seatingOf` (null for takeaway / parking), `follow_up` per §3.3 (was: no builder contract stated). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-20, owner 2026-11-11): a `kitchen_note` on a deal line is copied to every engine part of the deal (was: not stated; it stayed on the deal header, which no slip shows).
8. **Engine:** `checkOrderable(state, proposed)` → `409 STATION_PAUSED { station_id }`; a line with `out_of_stock_behaviour 'sold_out'` and available < needed → `409 ITEM_SOLD_OUT { item_id, available }` (O15); `reserveStock(state, proposed, dayStartMs, stockItems, configStockUnits)` → `{ drafts, from_stock, refused }` (as built, `stock.ts:159-185`); a `refused` line's `available` for `ITEM_SOLD_OUT` = min over its draws of `saleUnitsAvailable(availableStock(…) − the draw's units claimed by earlier lines of the payload, quantity_per_sale_unit)` (as `reserveStock` counts them); `planOrder(state, proposed, now, from_stock)` → groups, lines (route fields copied verbatim into `OrderPlaced.lines[]`, AD-17).
9. **Token** (O12): takeaway → `1 + MAX(token)` over `orders` of the `business_date`. ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): `nextToken` runs **only for a root order** (no `adds_to_order_id`); a takeaway addition's `OrderPlaced.token` and the command result's `token` are the **`adds_to_order_id` order's token**, read by the same statement that checks that order (step 2).
10. **Append** in this order (03a :615): lock drafts → `OrderPlaced` → `ItemAdded` × n in `order_item_ids` order → zone-served `ItemAdded`s → `StockReserved` × n → `ItemServed { order_item_ids: zone-served ids, source: 'zone' }` (if any) → `OrderContactSet { order_id, customer_name, phone }` (if either was given, O17) → the drafts of `alerts.raise` for `zone_stock_low` / `zone_stock_out` (§3.10).
10a. **Token slip** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT); 03c v1.0.16 row Q-TK-2): when `takeaway.print_slip === true` and the order is accepted, after the append and in the same transaction call 03c's **`queueTakeawaySlipOnSubmit(tx, { order_id, adds_to_order_id, token, business_date, placed_at, pickup_at_ms, groups: <the OrderPlaced.groups' target_land_ms / promise_ms>, device_id, queued_at: ctx.queuedAt, now })`**; it never throws (SAVEPOINT); statements + ≤ 3 (root) / + ≤ 4 (addition), 0 without the flag. The job commits and rolls back with the order, and D32 covers every retransmit (the same `command_id` returns the stored result, no second job).
11. **Result** `{ order_id, token | null, groups: [{ group_id, promise_ms, order_item_ids }], lines: [{ order_item_id, eta_ms | null, from_stock, zone_served }] }` — `promise_ms` only, never `target_land_ms` (03a #9). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT) (03c row Q-TK-3): + **`slip: { printed: boolean, job_id: string | null, skipped: null | 'setting_off' | 'slip_held' | 'replay_late' | 'no_printer' | 'failed' } | null`** (`null` without the flag; additive; the D32 stored response carries it). The client compares with its last preview and shows "time updated" when a promise moved ≥ 60 s (03a Q4).

**Statements:** 1 seating + lock (02a) + 1 ids (orders ∪ order_lines) + 1 `follow_up` (02a `seating_orders` ⋈ 02b) + 1 zone counts + 1 token + 1 `item_availability` + append batch (+ 1 per raised alert, 01d's bound); engine in memory. p99 < 60 ms at 12 lines. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): the pickup check (step 2b) adds no statement. ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): for a takeaway addition the `adds_to_order_id` check, which also reads that order's token and root (step 2), runs in place of the token statement (step 9).

### 3.3 Rounds, `follow_up`, open and billed

- Every submit is a new order (O1). `follow_up` (dine-in only) = an order of the **current** seating (02a `seating_orders`, P-1c) has a **food line** served (02b's own `order_lines`). Takeaway / parking: `false`.
- **Food line** (owner Q5; used below and in §3.5): an engine line (`isEngineLine`: a station, not a `deal`, 03a `plan.ts:17`) that is not zone-served and whose item is **not a ready item**. **Ready item** is an explicit item setting (`is_ready_item`, default false; 01a A-1, copied on `ItemAdded`, 01c C-1, stored as `order_lines.is_food` = engine line ∧ not zone-served ∧ ¬`is_ready_item`), set by the owner on water, drinks, packed items. Zone-served lines are never food. A pre-cooked item served from stock (reheated karahi, cold paye) is food unless the owner marks it ready. `cook_time_ms` is not used for this (01a requires it ≥ 1).
- **Table state (P-1d, final, owner Q5):** a seating's **live orders** stay every order with `cancelled = 0` (SEATED test, bill request, cleaning, merge / split guards — unchanged). Only the ORDERED / FOOD_ON_TABLE choice looks at **food orders** (`OrderPlaced.has_food`, E-1): FOOD_ON_TABLE when the seating has a live food order and every live food order has `served_any = 1`; ORDERED otherwise — so a table with only drinks / zone items shows ORDERED (never green), and a drinks round after mains leaves it green. `served_any = 1` is set only by 02b's **`OrderFoodServed { order_id }`**, appended after **every** `ItemServed` that serves at least one food line (tap, the estimate tick, a return's replacement; a repeat 1 → 1 is harmless). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-13, owner 2026-11-10): "serves at least one food line" = the `order_item_ids` of **this** `ItemServed` include a food line; an older food line that already has `served_at` (a returned karahi whose replacement still cooks) never counts, so a tap-served water then appends nothing (was: not said which lines count). `OrderFoodServed` also sets `has_food = 1` in 02a (a food line can reach an order that was cleared earlier). `OrderLinesAdded` (a return) still resets `served_any = 0` in 02a; when the returned line is **not** food (a water bottle sent back) **and the order has no live unserved food line**, 02b appends `OrderFoodCleared { food_served }` right after it; if a karahi of that order is still cooking, nothing is appended and the order waits for the karahi's `OrderFoodServed`. **`OrderFoodCleared { order_id, food_served: boolean }`** is appended when `RemoveItems`, `lineChangeDrafts` (04's voids) or a non-food return (gated as above) leave a live order with no live unserved food line; `food_served` (02b computes it from `order_lines`) → 02a sets `served_any = 1`, else `has_food = 0`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-14, owner 2026-11-11): `food_served` counts the order's served food lines with no live filter, so a food line voided after it was served still gives `true` (was: not stated). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-10, AD-13, owner 2026-11-10): both events come from the pure `foodDrafts(order_id, lines, after)` (§4.2): `lines` = the order's lines **after** the change (served_at set, a return line included); `after` = `{ kind: 'served'; order_item_ids } | 'change' | 'return'`; it returns drafts without time, actor or device (`{ stream_type, stream_id, event_type, schema_version, payload }`) and the caller adds the envelope (was: no contract stated).
- **Grouping** is always 02a's current `seating_orders` (read-only join, P-1c): `seating-orders`, `ordersOf`, `seatingTotals`, `follow_up`.
- **Billed:** every bill close writes `OrdersBilled { order_ids, bill_id }` (05, To 05; dine-in included — 05 reads `seating_orders` at close); the fold sets `billed_at` for every order type. The fold also clears effective unpaid hand-over approval (§6.19). A last close emitting `BillClosedEmpty` clears it through TK-15. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8)
- **Unbilled** ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-2): event **`OrdersUnbilled { order_ids: string[] (≥ 1), bill_id, alert_id?: string }`** (stream `order`, id = the first order id, `.strict()`; `alert_id` present on an approval), written by 05's reopen (§6.19). It joins 02b's `ORDER_EVENT_TYPES` and the client `ORDER_FRAMES`; its audience is the one of `OrdersBilled` (§6.19). Fold, for those orders: `billed_at = NULL` **and `day_closed_at = NULL`** and clears effective unpaid hand-over approval (§6.19). Every 02b / 04 command that refused `ORDER_BILLED` accepts them again. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8)
- **Day close:** `BusinessDayClosed` sets `day_closed_at` on **every open order** (any earlier date included) that is cancelled, billed, or whose lines are all served, removed or voided. An order that is neither cancelled nor billed and has a line still unserved stays open (a served-but-unbilled order leaves; 05 still bills it from `seating_orders`, run 4 m-3, W-1) and is shown under the next day with its **date beside its token** ("57 · 6 Nov", To 03b). ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling DC-2 (2026-11-27, found by the Tier 1 e2e lane): a **takeaway or parking order that is billed (paid) but still has a live unserved line also stays open** — it leaves only when handed over (all live lines served), cancelled or abandoned, never at the close just because it is billed. It is shown under the next day with its date beside its token, like an unbilled carried order, so the counter and the Pass still find it when the customer comes. Its stock stays reserved (03a PK-10, ruling DC-1). Dine-in keeps the rule above (billed → leaves). ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-5): 02b folds 05's **`OrdersCarriedOver { order_ids, … }`** (`hub` stream, 05 `CarryOverOrders`): `orders.carried_over = 1` for those orders (hub migration `0021`, §5). `applyDayClosed` **skips carried orders and clears the flag in the same fold**, so a carried order keeps `day_closed_at = NULL` and stays on the open-orders list next day; the carry applies to one close.
- **Open (lists)** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R3, G-1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-2, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)): an order is **open for the lists** (`open-orders`, §6.16; the catch-up part `orders`, §6.18) when it is not cancelled, not day-closed and — for **dine-in and parking** — not billed (unchanged); for **takeaway** — not billed **or** has a live line not yet **handed over**. A live line here is the day-close one: not a `deal` header, `units − removed_units − voided_units > 0`. **Handed over** (takeaway) = the line has `served_at` set, and for a takeaway that only ever comes from **`MarkServed`** (`served_source 'tap'`; the counter hands the bag to the customer, ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 owner review (OR-2): or the Pass does with "Handed to customer", 03b v1.0.16 §3.4): the `served_estimate` tick never serves a takeaway line (§3.7), zone lines never serve takeaway (§3.10), and a 03b Pass **`Handover`** of a takeaway is a Pass → counter hand-off that sets no `served_at` and keeps the order listed (**TK-Q12, answered by the owner 2026-11-24 (OR-2)**: neither the estimate nor a Pass `Handover` counts as handed to the customer; the Pass's own "Handed to customer" is a `MarkServed` and does). So a pay-first takeaway stays in the queue while it cooks and while its bag waits on the counter, and leaves when its last live line is handed over; a takeaway served before it is paid stays until it is billed (as today). **Day close is unchanged**: a billed order leaves at `BusinessDayClosed` even when a line is not yet handed over (a paid, uncollected takeaway; TK-Q7); `OrdersUnbilled` and carried orders as above. Was: open = not billed, not cancelled, not day-closed for every type; v1.0.25 first draft: "served = `served_at` set (tap, estimate or zone)". ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-7): The same open IDs define kiosk search membership (§8).

### 3.4 Units and the per-line money ledger

- **Units** of a line: `quantity` for `piece` lines; `1` for `g` / `ml` lines and while a weight is pending (= 03a :246); `quantity` for `deal` lines; a `deal_part` line's units per 01c :309 (piece part: part quantity × deal quantity; g / ml part: deal quantity); `option_child` lines follow their parent's units by `childQuantity`.
- **Refundable amount** (`refundable_minor`): `line_total_minor` for `item` and `option_child` lines; for a `deal_part` line, `allocated_price_minor` + its option charges (01c §3.4.8, :308–:311); `0` for a `deal` line (its money lives in its parts) and for return lines.
- **Unit states** (money): `live`, `live_comped`, `removed`, `voided`. A remade unit (`ItemReturned`) **stays live for money** (O20): it can still be comped or voided; `returned_units` is kept for the engine view only. `live_units = units − removed − voided`. An order with no live line is cancelled (§3.5).
- **Ledger.** `shares = allocateDealMinor(refundable_minor, [1 × units])`: every share is `q = floor(refundable / units)` except the last, `q + r` (01c, BigInt-safe). Units leave billing in order; the k-th unit to leave (k = `comped_units + removed_units + voided_units` before the action, returns not counted) takes `shares[k]`.
  - **Remove / void** take **uncomped** live units first. When they must take a comped unit, the unit leaves `comped_units` (and joins `removed_units` or `voided_units`) and its share leaves `comp_minor`: one unit's take-back = `comp_minor − q × (comped_units − 1)` (the remainder share, if a comp holds it, goes first; otherwise `q`), derived from the counters alone; on remove it moves to `removed_minor`; on void it moves to **`comp_voided_minor`** (O21), never to `refund_minor`.
  - **Comp** takes the next free shares.
  - Columns: `comp_minor`, `comp_voided_minor`, `removed_minor`, `refund_minor`. `billed_minor` of an `item` or `option_child` line = `refundable_minor − comp_minor − comp_voided_minor − removed_minor − refund_minor`. A **`deal_part` line's `billed_minor` = 0** (01c :303): its `refundable_minor` and counters only feed its deal line. A `deal` line's `billed_minor` = its `line_total_minor` − Σ over its parts of (`comp_minor + comp_voided_minor + removed_minor + refund_minor`) — the 01c rule "the deal total minus every refund already made on its parts".
  - Invariant per line: `comp_minor + comp_voided_minor + removed_minor + refund_minor ≤ refundable_minor` (CHECK); property test as 01c #119.
- **Deal headers:** a `deal` line is never in `EngineState` (03a `apply-event.ts:445-447`). Its stage and served state are **derived from its parts** (01c :306): at stage 1 when every part is; served when every live part is served; live while any part has a live unit. `MarkServed` never names a header; day close, `ready n / m` and "no live line" (§3.5) look at parts only. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-29, owner 2026-11-11): a `MarkServed` that names a deal header → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (path `lines[i].order_item_ids[j]`) (was: "never names a header", no answer stated).
- **Deal actions:** removing, voiding or comping `n` **deal** units applies `n` portions to every part (per-portion units, 01c :309) in the same transaction. Removing deal units is allowed only when **every part line it touches is at stage 1** (§3.5), else `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE`. **g/ml deal parts:** the engine counts a g/ml line as 1 unit (03a :244, `plan.ts:23-26`), so removing fewer than all live units of a deal with a g/ml part (or of a g/ml `deal_part` alone) while that part is open in the engine (stage before `served`) is refused `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { path: 'lines.N.units', reason: 'gml_deal_part_partial' }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-17, owner 2026-11-11): 400, the foundation §6.2 status (was: `422 VALIDATION_FAILED`)) — remove the whole deal and re-add (run 4 M-1). A single part's units can be voided by 04 (01c) and removed at stage 1 by 02b; a part alone is never comped (comp the deal unit).
- **Cascade to children:** a remove, void or comp of `n` parent units applies to each `option_child` the units `childQuantity` gives.
- **Cascades are capped** (01c :312): each part or child line gives `min(per-portion units × n, its live units)` for remove / void and `min(…, live − comped units)` for comp; the parent's **last** live unit takes every remaining part / child unit.
- **Null-quantity option child** ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.13 (owner 2026-11-12, 04 Q-19): an `option_child` added to a submitted line later (`option_quantity: null`, 01c §3.4.8 Q4) is **left alone by a partial** parent void / remove / comp (`cascadeUnits` → 0: no event, not a touched id for the line guard); the parent's **last** unit (comp: last uncomped live unit) or a whole-line action gives it `min(cap, its units left)` (cap as above). The rule is tied to the line kind: it applies **only to an `option_child` of an `item` parent whose `option_quantity` is null**. The ratio rule (child units = per-parent-unit units × parent units, a multiple of the parent's units, else a programming error) and the per-portion cascade apply to every other child: placed option children (`option_quantity` set) and **every deal part** (a `deal` parent), whatever its `option_quantity` — deal parts are always written with `option_quantity: null` (`build-submit.ts:186`; 01c Q4 "null on every other kind") and keep 01c N1 / :309 unchanged. `LedgerRef` carries `option_quantity: number \| null`, read from the `orders` projection (`order_lines.option_quantity`, §5) in the row each caller already reads: `readOrder` (`RemoveItems`, 04 `VoidItems` / `void_approval`) and the `CompLine` / `applyComp` line read — no extra statement. Its own unit voids / removes / comps are unchanged.
- **Cascades are events per line:** every cascaded line gets its own `ItemRemoved` / `ItemVoided` / `LineComped` with its own units and money; folds never cascade; a parent's `LineComped.value_minor` is its own share only (the command result may sum); inside the comp `apply` every `LineComped` carries the alert id (01d `checkAlertIds`).
- **Void lock (line guard)** ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3): a line in an open void request is locked. Every 02b command that would change a line's units or money asks the **line guard** with the touched lines it **planned** (the line, its cascaded children / parts with ≥ 1 unit): `registerLineGuard((db, order_id, touched_ids) → { alert_id, order_item_id } | null)` in `apps/hub/src/orders/line-guard.ts` (02a merge-guard pattern: `registerLineGuard`, `resetLineGuard`, `checkLineGuard`; unregistered → never blocks, 0 statements; registered → 1 statement). A hit → `409 VOID_PENDING { alert_id, order_item_id }`. Asked by `CompLine` (every grant), `applyComp` (the approve), `RemoveItems` (its `planRemove` ids) and `SetLineWeight` (the line itself), each **after** its own `404` / `409` checks and **before** scope (C8). 04 registers it (04 §6.6).
  - ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3), which supersedes the signature above: `registerLineGuard` **appends** to an ordered list (04 first, then 05); the first non-null result wins. The guard input is `(db, order_id, touched: [{ order_item_id, units_after, comped_after, base_after_minor, money_changes }])`; **every** caller (`RemoveItems`, `VoidItems`, `CompLine`, `RemoveComp`, `SetLineWeight`) passes, from its own plan or ledger result, the planned live units after its change (`units_after`), `comped_after` and `base_after_minor` (`CompLine`, `RemoveComp`: the new `comped_after`; `SetLineWeight` and any price change: `money_changes: true`); 04's guard reads only the ids. The result widens to `{ code: 'VOID_PENDING' | 'LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT', details } | null` (04's result maps to today's `VOID_PENDING { alert_id, order_item_id }` unchanged). 05's guard refuses when `units_after` is below the units closed splits invoiced for the line, or `comped_after > units_after − closed units`, or `money_changes` is true on a line with any closed units, or `base_after − Σ closed shares < 0`, or `≠ 0` while `units_after` equals the closed units (a residual on zero open units): `409 LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT { order_item_id, bill_id, split_no }`, only on a not-CLOSED bill. The guard is called **once per command** with every touched line as the list `touched`, and 05's closed-units check is **1 statement for the whole list** (one read of `closed_split_units` for those ids); each registered guard answers in 1 statement, so a command's line-guard cost is +1 per registered guard.
  - ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3, R-33): **parent deal entry.** When a **`deal_part`** line is touched **without its deal** (removed at stage 1 or voided by 04 on its own; a part is never comped alone, so comp and `RemoveComp` never take this path), the caller adds the **parent deal line's entry** to `touched`: `{ order_item_id: <the deal line>, units_after: the deal's live units (unchanged), comped_after: unchanged, base_after_minor: the deal's `billed_minor` after the change, recomputed from its parts by the deal rule above (`line_total_minor` − Σ parts' money out, the planned part included), money_changes: true }`, computed from the caller's own plan and the parts it already read (no extra statement). So a guard that refuses a money change on a line with closed units (05's `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`) sees the deal whose money moves. **An `order_item_id` may repeat in one `touched` list** (e.g. 04's `void-preview` judging a deal and one of its parts, both entries naming the deal); every entry is judged on its own, in list order, and the first refusal wins; a guard reads its rows once for the distinct ids (05: still 1 statement). Tests 53, 54.
- **Weights:** `SetLineWeight` on a line with any unit out of billing → `409 LINE_ALREADY_SETTLED`. The `LineWeightSet` fold sets `refundable_minor = line_total_minor` (a weighed line starts at 0) and, ⚠️ Changed by 05 (R-7, R-17), `unit_price_minor = line_total_minor` (a weighed line has 1 unit; NULL until then, §5).
- **`lineShares(line)`** (pure, exported): input `{ refundable_minor, units, comped_units, removed_units, voided_units, comp_minor }`; returns `next_share_minor(n)` (shares `k … k+n−1`) and `comped_take_back_minor(n)` (the take-back rule above applied `n` times). 04 uses it for every void (To 04).

### 3.5 Remove at stage 1 (`RemoveItems`) and `OrderCancelled`

- **Stage 1** (F §4.2): the `EngineState` line has `stage === 'added'` (fired or not, O16); any other stage (`cooking`, `done`, `served`, `returned`) — including a Done or Served line with no Start — is past stage 1. Past it → `409 ITEM_WRONG_STAGE { order_item_id, stage }` (the client offers 04's void; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-24): disabled, "Comes with module 04", until 04 is built). A line of an order no longer in the engine (abandon or exit, O16) → `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE { stage: 'out_of_engine' }`. A fired line's removal is printed as a CANCELLED slip by 03b (To 03b).
- **Zone-served line** (checked by the zone rule only, **before** any engine-stage check, so a zone-only round is undoable): removable within `zone_undo_ms` (branch setting, default 300 000) of its `ItemAdded` — a check of the command only; the fold always adds the units back to the zone count. Later → `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE` (manual Count, §8). F-1 lists this exception.
- Each entry `{ order_item_id, units? }`: keyed entries, a repeated `order_item_id` → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (C6); absent `units` = all live units; `units > live_units` → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-17, owner 2026-11-11): 400, the foundation §6.2 status (was: `422 VALIDATION_FAILED`)); partial on `piece`, `deal` and `deal_part` lines (§3.4 units). A `deal_part` alone may be removed (its share per §3.4, O19).
- **Drafts:** per line `ItemRemoved { order_item_id, units }` (03a Q-4) + `stockEffects(ItemRemoved)` releasing **that share** (Q-4) → stale comp requests **of the changed lines** closed (§3.9, one `alerts.listOpen` for the order; ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-4): only open comp requests whose `order_item_id` is a line in the changes are judged) → `OrderFoodCleared { order_id, food_served }` when the order keeps a live line but no live unserved food line (§3.3) → `OrderCancelled { order_id }` when no live line is left. A zone undo that lifts a count closes the zone alerts `done` by the §3.10 rule (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.3, AD-11: `zone_stock_out` once above 0, `zone_stock_low` once above its threshold; was: a lift above the threshold closes `zone_stock_low` / `zone_stock_out`) (one `alerts.listOpen` for `zone:<id>`, only when a zone line is in the payload; run 4 m-6).
- **Export** `lineChangeDrafts(ctx, order_id, changes: { order_item_id, units_out }[], opts?: { stale_comps: 'close' | 'keep' })` (⚠️ Changed by 04 (G-23): the text said `tx`; AD-9 already made it `ctx`) returns `OrderCancelled` (if the order is emptied), `OrderFoodCleared { food_served }` (§3.3; it computes `food_served` from the lines it already reads) and the stale comp-request closes; 2 statements (lines, `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'comp', subject: order })`); 04 calls it in every void transaction (To 04). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-9, owner 2026-11-10): the signature is `lineChangeDrafts(ctx: CommandCtx, order_id, changes)`; the spec's `tx` reads as `ctx.tx`, and the comp-request closes carry the caller's actor and device (02a `requireLock` precedent) (was: `lineChangeDrafts(tx, order_id, changes)`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-14, owner 2026-11-11): its `food_served` follows §3.3 (a food line voided after its serve still counts as served) (was: not stated).
  - ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-4; G-15): **mode** `opts.stale_comps` (default `'close'`). `'keep'` judges **no** comp request and skips the comp `listOpen` read (1 statement: the lines) — used only by 04's void `apply`, where a foreign `AlertClosed` would break 01d's `checkAlertIds` (K-1). The option is on **`changeDrafts(ctx, order_id, before, changes, opts?)`** as well, because `RemoveItems` calls `changeDrafts` directly; `'keep'` skips `staleCompCloses`. **Changed lines only:** `changeDrafts` judges only open comp requests whose `order_item_id` is a line in `changes` — `freeOf` returns `undefined` for any id not in `changes` (as `CompLine`'s own `staleCompCloses` call already does). An already-stale comp request elsewhere on the order stays open until its own line changes or it is answered (01d refuses the answer if it no longer fits). Applies to `RemoveItems` too. The `LineChangeDrafts` type (`orders/contracts.ts`) gains the option.
  - ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-4): a void calls `lineChangeDrafts` **once per command**; in an approval every returned draft carries the request's `alert_id`; per-line dispositions are 04's (04 V3).
  - ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-7): `registerOrderCancelledHook((ctx, order_id, alert_id: string | null) → EventDraft[])` (`orders/line-change.ts`; a **list**, registered at startup; unregistered → no change). **`changeDrafts`** — the shared core of `RemoveItems` and `lineChangeDrafts` — calls every registered hook when it emits `OrderCancelled` (direct paths and 04's void `apply` alike) and appends the drafts they return; `alert_id = ctx.alert?.alert_id` inside an `apply` (`AlertApplyCtx`), else `null`, and each returned draft carries it (01d `checkAlertIds`). 05's hook returns `BillClosedEmpty` when the order's bill (any bill with a `bills` row) is now all cancelled with every split balance 0.

### 3.6 Weighed lines (`SetLineWeight`)

A line with `requires_weighing` and `quantity null` is pending. `SetLineWeight { order_item_id, quantity }` (S59 steps of 50, 50–50 000) → `LineWeightSet { order_item_id, quantity, line_total_minor }`, total from the **`ItemAdded` copy** (01c M7). A correction is allowed until the bill closes, unless a unit left billing (`LINE_ALREADY_SETTLED`). ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3): after `LINE_ALREADY_SETTLED` and before scope, the line guard (§3.4) is asked with the line itself → `409 VOID_PENDING`. Non-weighed → `409 LINE_NOT_WEIGHED`; billed → `409 ORDER_BILLED`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-27, owner 2026-11-11): a return line (money 0, §3.4), even one that copied `requires_weighing`, → `409 LINE_NOT_WEIGHED { order_item_id }` (was: not stated; the `LineWeightSet` fold would have given it money).

### 3.7 Served (`MarkServed`) and the estimate

- `MarkServed { lines: [{ order_id, order_item_ids }] }`; no lock (D39). A line must be **fired** (in a dispatched load, or started, or done), or belong to an order that has left the engine (O16; no `stockEffects` for it); an unfired line → `409 ITEM_WRONG_STAGE { order_item_id, stage: 'added' }`, nothing written. Already served → skipped (`result.skipped`). Removed / voided → `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-29, owner 2026-11-11): a deal header named in `order_item_ids` → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (path `lines[i].order_item_ids[j]`, §3.4); a **no-station line** (never in `EngineState`) can be marked served, with no fired check (was: not stated for lines the engine never holds).
- One `ItemServed { order_item_ids, source: 'tap', served_at? }` per order, followed by `OrderFoodServed { order_id }` after every `ItemServed` that serves at least one food line (§3.3), (`served_at` = the accepted `queued_at` when the command was queued, §3.12; 03a v1.2 Q-6 additive, the engine and 02b use `min(served_at, recorded_at)` as for `ItemStarted`) + `stockEffects` drafted from folded state, array form across the whole command (03a AD-29 Q7).
- **Estimated Served** (G0-6): when 03b ships `Handover`, 02b registers tick `served_estimate` (`due` = 1 indexed statement for handovers older than `served_estimate_ms` with unserved lines; `intents` = `ItemServed { source: 'estimated' }` per order + `stockEffects` + `OrderFoodServed` when it serves a food line). Not registered before (O14). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-2, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): the tick **skips takeaway orders** (its `due` / `intents` filter on `orders.order_type <> 'takeaway'`, in the statement it already runs over 03b's `handoversDue` result, 0 extra statements): a takeaway is handed to the customer at the counter (`MarkServed`), so an estimate never takes a paid takeaway off the queue (§3.3). Dine-in and parking unchanged.


### 3.7a Unpaid hand-over request ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 owner review (OR-7)

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): OR-7 supersedes the client override. The staff customer hand-over path uses the transactional check in §6.4 (D2). Ordinary serving keeps the R2 / 05 B16 contract. The pilot adds no `TAKEAWAY_UNPAID` code.

- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): **When.** A current approval is required to hand over the last live line of a named takeaway or parking order with `bill.state = "due"` and `due_minor > 0`. Before OR-7 ships, staff screens block full hand-over of those orders. A partial serve must leave an unserved live line in each Due, unapproved order it touches, including a multi-order token. Show “Take payment first” and “Ask a manager” for a blocked action. Parking is included (TK-Q18), with `token: null` and identifier `PARKING`.
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): **Ask.** ~~Send `RequestUnpaidHandover { order_id }` for the named Due order. The Pass uses its card’s order. The grouped counter starts with the newest eligible Due order in the attempted send and identifies it. One pending request per order.~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9): Send `RequestUnpaidHandover { order_id }` naming any Due order of an unpaid **bill**: the request covers that bill's whole due and every live takeaway / parking order on it. One pending request per bill; a token whose orders are on two bills gets two requests (one per bill). The Pass card and the counter ask once per unpaid bill — one tap when the token has one unpaid bill, a per-bill sheet when it has two or more. 01d routes it to branch-scope holders of `hand-over-unpaid` (Owner and Manager by default), presence-filtered, excluding the requester. A holder answers with PIN on their own listed device. The requester sees “Waiting for a manager…” and may withdraw. With no holder present, show “Waiting — find a manager.” A second requester seeing the same pending ID sees “Already waiting for a manager” and learns the outcome from the order/bill refresh; the personal result belongs to the original requester.
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): **Approve.** Record `UnpaidHandoverApproved { order_id, alert_id, approved_by_staff_id, due_minor }` on approval, with due verified at approval time. ~~The projection keeps the time and approved amount for the named order. A sibling, including an addition already on the same bill, needs its own approval.~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9): the approval covers the request's bill: one `UnpaidHandoverApproved` per order of the bill (the request's `order_ids` still live on that bill), each at the bill's due; the projection keeps the time and amount on each. An order added to the bill afterwards raises its due above the approved amount, which ends the approval (below). Effective approval ends on bill close/reopen or when due rises above the approved amount before hand-over. A later drop cannot revive it. Keep the original event for audit and replay (§6.19).
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3): **Reject / stale.** Reject writes no order approval. Requests have no automatic expiry and end on answer, withdrawal or an owner close. A changed due makes the old card obsolete (§6.4a, §6.12a, §6.19).
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3): **Close.** In the owning writer transaction close the still-open request `obsolete` and send 01d’s `request_result` to its original requester: (a) `MarkServed` leaves the named order with no live unserved non-deal line, note `handed_over`; (b) cancellation of that order, note `order_cancelled`, including 04’s `stale_comps: "keep"` approval after cancel folds and outside its restricted apply drafts; (c) 05 last bill close (including empty close), note `bill_closed`; (d) 05 day close, note `day_closed`, even for carried orders. The first close wins. An answer after it gets 01d’s existing `409 REQUEST_NOT_PENDING` with standard closed-request details. A changed amount also closes as specified in §6.19.

### 3.8 Release, boost, return

- `ReleaseItems { order_ids, released, expedite }` (sets) → `planRelease` (03a §3.8; it fills `alert_id` per group from memory) → `ItemsReleased` per order; every distinct non-null `alert_id` `planRelease` returned is closed `done` with one `alerts.closeMany` (03a To 02 #7). D12's goes-with pre-ticks are the **client's**: the release sheet pre-ticks goes-with partners; a partner that is not done goes in **`expedite`** (`planRelease` keeps only done lines in `released`, `slip.ts:686-716`); `released` holds only ticked done lines. No extra read. On a joined table the card's "Send what's ready" sends one `ReleaseItems` with every order of the card, so each warning's `alert_id` is closed in that one `closeMany` (O18).
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1): `BoostOrder { order_id }` → `OrderBoosted`; missing from the engine → `409 ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE`. D1 (Q-PK-9) keeps `EngineOrder.pickup_at_ms` and `orders.pickup_at_ms` and sets `boosted = true`.
