Effective scheduling pickup is `boosted ? null : pickup_at_ms` in every planning path (preview, fold, boost, requeue, priority, later additions to the engine order, PR-4, R4 hold and held-slip selection). A boost releases and rebooks its own unfired held FIFO work at the earliest feasible gap; other orders’ FIFO stays fixed. The slip set keeps sync groups and includes eligible immediate groups only while effective pickup is non-null. A drinks-only held slip resolves; any unresolved sync group stays open and no new held-drink warning is scheduled. Readiness stays whole-order, held lines included. 03a keeps state v6 and replay rules and drops PK-1’s numeric placement guarantee while retaining the gap and stored-promise rule.
- `ReturnToKitchen { order_item_id, return_order_item_id, mode: 'fix' | 'remake' }` (03a §3.8a; whole served line; no note): served, its order in the engine, not zone-served, **not already remade** (`returned_units > 0` → `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE { stage: 'returned' }`; the client offers Return on the replacement line; if the replacement was removed or voided, the dish is ordered again as a new round — the engine never re-returns a `returned` line), no open return (`409 RETURN_ALREADY_OPEN`). Build a one-line `ProposedOrder` (`return_of`, `cook_ms_override` = the item's `adjust_time_ms` for `fix`, money 0, `line_kind 'item'`, links null, `options []` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.8 (owner 2026-11-11, B3-4): `options` = the served line's plain options (`ItemAdded.options`, every `price_minor` / `charge_minor` 0) **and** its live option children **with no station**, each as a plain option entry (`option_id`, the option's `modifier_group_id` from config, `name`, `quantity` = the child's `option_quantity`, money 0); option children **with a station** are their own lines and are not copied), `quantity` copied) and call `planOrder` for `groups` and `lines` → `OrderLinesAdded` + its `ItemAdded` (+ `ItemReturned` for `remake`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-15, owner 2026-11-11): the return line's `ItemAdded` carries the optional `return_of` (`ItemAdded` v1, additive; fix and remake), and the `orders` projection folds `order_lines.return_of` from it (§5, §6.19) (was: `return_of` only on the `ProposedOrder` line, with no event the projection could fold). ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling 2026-11-09 (E-4, 02b v1.0.2): the `OrderLinesAdded` always carries `order_header` (03a §4.2: `order_type`, `table_key`, `table_id`, `follow_up`, `pickup_at_ms`, `placed_by`), copied from the order's `EngineOrder` in `EngineState` (the in-engine check above already holds it; `placed_by` = the envelope `actor_id` of its `OrderPlaced`, as `EngineOrder.placed_by`, 03a §4.2), so no extra statement and no new column (`orders` has no `table_id` or `pickup_at_ms`). 02b does not guess engine state: the engine uses the header only for an order it does not hold, which the in-engine check above makes unreachable today. ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling 2026-11-09 (E-7, 02b v1.0.2): **an order does not leave the engine when it is served**; it stays for 30 min after its last line is served (03a §3.3 exit), so a dish sent back inside that window is returned normally. After the window, `ReturnToKitchen` is refused `409 ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE` with **nothing appended and no alert** (unchanged: 03a G4-1 #7, test 101; test 25 here), and the client offers the way out (§8): **"Send it as a new item"**, which is the normal new round (O1) with that item, plus "Comp it?" on the new line through the usual `CompLine` when the dish should be free. If the engine ever re-creates an order from the header, its group ids restart at `order_id:0` (03a §3.3 E-4), so 02b keys nothing per order on `group_id`; and later events on lines the engine never held are logged as absent ids, which is expected (02p 15b review M-4). Return lines are never billed or comped. The remade line stays billable (O20); a pending comp request on it stays valid. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-3): Copy the served point if active. If archived, route this return to an active point by §3.2 step 6 and record it in `OrderLinesAdded.lines[]` (D7).

### 3.9 Comps (`CompLine`, kind `comp`)

- `CompLine { order_item_id, units, reason_code, note? }` on a submitted live line (O13), remade lines included (O20); not a return line, not a `deal_part` (comp the deal unit, §3.4), not zone-served pending undo → `409 COMP_NOT_ALLOWED { reason }`. `note` is required when `reason_code = 'other'` (E0 AC3: reason required) → else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`. `units ≤ live_units − comped_units` else 400 (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-17, owner 2026-11-11): 400, the foundation §6.2 status; was: `422`). Pending weight → `409 LINE_WEIGHT_PENDING`. Billed → `ORDER_BILLED`. ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3; G-12): after these `404` / `409` / `400` checks, `CompLine` runs its pure `compDrafts` / `planComp` (0 statements) **before** `checkScope`, then asks the line guard (§3.4) with the **plan's** touched ids (0-unit cascade lines dropped), for every grant → `409 VOID_PENDING`; then scope (C8).
- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-19): the client sends each config chip (`comp_reason_chips`, owner-edited labels) as `reason_code` = its label, and adds **one fixed "Other" chip** that sends `'other'` with the required note; the cloud default list has no "Other" (01a v2.4.4). The approver sees `details.reason` as sent. Was: chips and `'other'` not tied.
- `value_minor` (the request's `details.amount` and the command result) = Σ over **every line the comp touches** (the line, its option children, a deal's parts) of that line's next free shares; each `LineComped` carries only its own line's value (§3.4). A deal comp therefore shows the deal's value, never Rs 0.
- `defineCommand` with `approvable: true` (00 :470). Grant `allowed` → `LineComped { …, requester_staff_id: caller, alert_id: null, approver_staff_id: null }`. Grant `requires_approval` → `alerts.raise(ctx, { kind: 'comp', subject: { stream_type: 'order', stream_id: order_id }, target: { kind: 'answerers' }, values: { order_id, order_item_id, units, reason_code, note: note ?? null }, details: { item, quantity: units, amount: value_minor, reason }, display, dedupe_key: 'comp:' + order_item_id, requester_staff_id: caller, request: { approver_role_ids: ctx.permission.approver_role_ids } })`; result `{ alert_id, pending: true, value_minor }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-11): `value_minor` added, 0 statements; was `{ alert_id, pending: true }`); `deduped: true` → `409 COMP_PENDING { alert_id }`.
- `apply` (approve): read-only re-check; ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3; G-12): `applyComp` (`orders/commands/comp-line.ts`) plans, then asks the line guard with the plan's ids (`409 VOID_PENDING` refuses the approve; 1 → 2 statements with a registered guard; ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3): +1 per further registered guard, §3.4); returns `LineComped { …, note: alert.values.note, requester_staff_id: alert.requester_staff_id, alert_id, approver_staff_id: answer.answerer_staff_id }` with `value_minor` recomputed from the current ledger; reject → `[]`.
- **Stale requests:** ⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-4): judged per changed line only (§3.5 `changeDrafts`); a void `apply` uses `stale_comps: 'keep'` and closes none. Every write that leaves `live_units − comped_units < requested units` — remove, void (through `lineChangeDrafts`), a direct `allowed` comp by someone else, order cancelled — closes the open `comp:<order_item_id>` request `obsolete` with a note in the same transaction (one `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'comp', subject: { stream_type: 'order', stream_id: order_id } })` per order); 01d tells the requester. At bill close 05 closes the bill's open comp requests (To 05).
- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-4): **Remove comp.** Event **`LineCompRemoved { order_item_id, units, comp_minor, reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }`** (stream `order`; the note text is in 05's `bill_texts`, never in the event), written only by 05's `RemoveComp` on an **unbilled** line with `comped_units ≥ units`. Fold: `comped_units −= units`, `comp_minor −= comp_minor` (never below 0). Export `planCompRemoval(line, units) → { units, comp_minor }` (the inverse of `planComp`'s per-unit value; 01c §3.4.8 cascades). A comp is therefore undone **only before billing, by 05's `RemoveComp` with approval** (§12.3 Q2).
- Stock: a comped line was cooked and served — no stock event (owner A).

### 3.10 Zones: routing and stock

- **Config (01a row A-1):** `zone_item_routes (zone_id, item_id, station_id)`, `zone_stock_items (zone_id, item_id, threshold)` in snapshot V4 `floors[].zones[].routes[]` / `.stock_items[]`. Zones are **flat** (01a v2.2). A table without a zone: no zone routes, no zone stock.
- **Routing:** the primary table's zone route for the item, else the branch route. Dine-in only.
- **Counts:** projection `zone_stock (zone_id, item_id, on_hand)` changed by `ZoneStockAdded` (+), `ZoneStockSet` (=), zone-served `ItemAdded` (−), `ItemRemoved` of a zone-served line (+, always; the undo window is the command's check only). Voids (04) never change it.
- **Refill** `AddZoneStock { zone_id, item_id, quantity 1–500 }`: a zone item of that zone; for each of the item's day draws (`dayDraws`), `draw × quantity` leaves branch stock: short → `409 STOCK_INSUFFICIENT { stock_item_id, available }`; drafts `StockTransferredOut { stock_item_id, quantity, zone_id }` per draw (03a Q-5; 01a A-1 adds it to `STOCK_VERSION_EVENTS`, the stock-frame audience and the boot fold lists, so tiles update within 5 s) + `ZoneStockAdded { zone_id, item_id, quantity, transfers }`. Needs an open day.
- **Count** `SetZoneStock { zone_id, item_id, on_hand 0–5 000 }` → `ZoneStockSet { zone_id, item_id, on_hand, previous }` (`transfers` none; 08 counts zone-served draws beyond transferred quantities, To 08).
- **Alerts:** a count that moves from above `threshold` to ≤ it raises `zone_stock_low` (dedupe `zone_low:<zone_id>:<item_id>`; target `{ kind: 'permission', permission: 'manage-zone-stock' }` — the refill person, whatever their role, owner Q5 (b) — plus `managers` at step 0; 01d's `permission` target is branch-wide, so every refill holder of the branch is told); a count reaching 0 raises `zone_stock_out` (dedupe `zone_out:<zone_id>:<item_id>`), even if the low alert is open. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.3 (owner ruling 2026-11-10, AD-11): a count above 0 closes `zone_stock_out` `done`; a count above the threshold closes `zone_stock_low` `done` (each on its own; was: a count back above the threshold closes both `done`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): the zone path's statement count in `SubmitOrder` (§6.1) **includes** 01d's `alerts.listOpen` for the zone alerts, which is stricter than the §6 Common rule "exclude 01d's own bounds" (was: not stated).

### 3.11 Token and business day

`orders.business_date` = the open day's `business_date`. Token per §3.2 step 9; ~~unique `(business_date, token)`~~ ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): **not unique** — a takeaway addition keeps its root order's token, so the index `orders_token_day (business_date, token)` is non-unique (§5; was: unique `(business_date, token)`). A day closed and reopened the same date continues the numbering. ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): **token rule:** a token is a display identifier only; every lookup is by `order_id` / `bill_key`. The call sites that show it (03b `kds/commands/ready.ts:89-130`, `kds/commands/handover.ts`, `kds/details.ts` incl. `return_of`'s `ro.token`; 03c `print/render/jobs.ts:58-61`, `:140-223`; 02b `orders/queries/{open-orders,order-view}.ts`, `orders/exports.ts`; client `pass/cards/PassCard.tsx`, `staff/orders/lists/OpenOrdersRow.tsx`, `staff/orders/view/OrderView.tsx`, `staff/orders/queue/QueuedSentCard.tsx:28`) read it by order id and need no change. A future token search returns the bill or the latest open order of that token. No "adds to #" print.

### 3.12 Device command queue

- **Queueable command types (client and hub):** `SubmitOrder`, `MarkServed` (02b), `RequestBill`, `CleanTable` (02a, declared `queueable` by P-1f). `CallWaiter` is **not** queued (a call 2 h late is noise). Nothing else is ever queued.
- **Device:** written to the queue (tablet SQLite in 9b's app; kiosk / phone IndexedDB) **before** the first send, with `command_id`, `queued_at` = the device's estimate of **hub time** (device clock + the offset from the last `hub_time` frame, 01a §4.5), `session_token`, `staff_id`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-1): one `QueueStore` / `DraftStore` interface in `apps/client` (no Capacitor import); its **IndexedDB** implementation is used now by every hub-served client (kiosk, phone, browser tablet); the SQLite implementation comes with 01d 9b (T1) (was: "tablet SQLite in 9b's app; kiosk / phone IndexedDB"). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-2): the stored entry holds `staff_id`, **not** `session_token` — the token stays in memory only (00 §8.1 unchanged); same-person drain works within the page's life; **after a reload or app restart every queued entry goes to the queue log as "Send again as {current person}"** (same `command_id` and ids → a command already committed gets its stored 2xx, the hub rule below) (was: `session_token` stored with the entry). Replay in `queued_at` order. **The queue is drained before a new login is offered on the device**: the login screen shows "2 orders waiting to send" and sends them first; a staff member can still log in after a failed drain; the old commands then wait in the queue log for "Send again" (see the hub rule). Removed on 2xx or 4xx; a 4xx goes to the **queue log** (what, when, why, "Discard", "Copy to a new order" — the copy mints new ids), except `426 CLIENT_UPGRADE_REQUIRED` (the command stays queued and is retried after the app update) and `403 DEVICE_REVOKED` (the queue is kept when the token is wiped, run 4 m-7, W-4). "Order submitted" only after a 2xx (E5 AC4). Once a cart's command is queued, the cart is frozen; `ORDER_ITEM_ID_IN_USE { order_id }` for the device's own pending order means it was already accepted: refetch `seating-orders` and show it as sent.
- **Hub (00 row H-1, simplified by the owner 2026-11-07): the idle clock runs only while the device is connected.** The hub keeps in memory, per device, its open sockets (session or alert mode) and `paused_at`. When the device's last socket closes (including the hub terminating it after a missed pong, `PONG_MS` 30 s), `paused_at = now`; at boot every device starts paused at `HubStarted.hub_down_at` (00 §5.8, test 71; boot time on a first boot). At the device's next contact — any authenticated request or socket connect — **before** M46's idle check, its staff session's in-memory `last_activity_at` moves forward by `now − paused_at` and `paused_at` is cleared; **if the device still has no open socket after the shift, `paused_at = now` again** (an HTTP request that arrives before the socket, then a second drop, keeps the clock paused — S-1). The sweeper skips a paused device's session, and its 15 s save (M5) also writes the shifted `last_activity_at` of paused devices, so a hub crash mid-outage does not count the time before the crash as idle (S-2). So a session never times out while its tablet cannot reach the hub, and on reconnect the queue replays in that live session through the normal pipeline (M19 fast path, session, M39, permission read now). The shift is not activity (M59 unchanged: pongs and polls never count). **Cap:** the hub also keeps in memory **`paused_since`** — set when the device's last socket closes (at boot = `hub_down_at`) and cleared **only** when a socket of the device opens; S-1's re-pause and the M5 save move only `paused_at`. A session whose device has `now − paused_since` > `queue_auto_send_max_ms` (default 14 400 000, 4 h) is ended `idle_timeout` by the sweeper (run 4 M-2) — a tablet left off overnight does not keep yesterday's login. **Ended or unknown session** (`logout`, `replaced`, `taken_over`, `forced`, `credentials_changed`, `hub_restored`, the cap → `401 SESSION_EXPIRED`; a token unknown after a restored backup → `401 SESSION_INVALID`): never accepted; the command goes to the queue log with **"Send again"** (after the next login; same `command_id` and ids, sent as the person now logged in, named on the button — a command that was in fact committed gets its stored response (M19 replay, 2xx, `Idempotent-Replay`) and is shown as sent; `ORDER_ITEM_ID_IN_USE` only after the 7-day `processed_commands` prune, also shown as sent) and "Discard". **`X-Queued-At`:** a replay of a queueable command sends `X-Queued-At: <queued_at>`; the hub clamps it to `[started_at − 120 000, now]` and ignores it on other commands; it sets `OrderPlaced.sent_after_reconnect = true` (E-1) and `ItemServed.served_at = queued_at` (§3.7; 02b's fold uses `min(served_at, recorded_at)` like the engine, Q-6). **Shared tablets:** the device's own idle lock (§8) logs the person out after the idle TTL whether or not it is connected, so a paused hub session is not a way to use someone else's login. **Accepted residual:** a client that ignores its own lock can use a paused session after reconnect until it idles out — the same as holding a live token; bounded by the 4 h cap. No new column, index, event or error code.
- **Drafts in progress** stay on the device per (device, staff) and survive restarts.

### 3.13 Totals

`seatingTotals(db, seating_id)` = Σ `billed_minor` (§3.4; pending weight = 0, return lines 0) over the lines of the seating's live orders, joined through 02a's `seating_orders` (P-1c). 1 statement.

### 3.14 Permissions and scopes

| Command | Permission | Scope check (C8, last) |
|---|---|---|
| `SubmitOrder` | `take-order` | ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.5 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): dine-in: 02a's rule — the caller must pass `requireLock` on the existing seating: the holder, or a `take-order-anywhere` grant whose scope covers the floor (02a v1.1.4 §3.2, §3.7, §4.2; else `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`); take-order `my_floors` is checked only where 02a checks it (`OpenTable` on a FREE table, "with the floor", 02a §3.2); no extra `my_floors` check on every submit; takeaway / parking: none (was: "dine-in: subsumed by `requireLock` (holder, or `take-order-anywhere`) plus `my_floors` / `branch` on the table; takeaway / parking: none", and v1.0.4 (AD-36): "`my_floors` is checked on **every** dine-in submit against the floor of the named `table_id` (the person's floors must cover it, else `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'outside_scope', table_ids }`); `branch` passes; `own_tables` is the holder rule `requireLock` already applied. 02a `OpenTable` (02a v1.1.4 §3.2, owner note Q1) checks `my_floors` “with the floor” only when it opens a FREE table, so the two texts differ; one rule for both is left to the next 02a and 02b bumps") |
| `RemoveItems` | `take-order` | dine-in: as `SubmitOrder` on the current table; takeaway / parking: `own_tables` and `my_floors` = placed by the person, `branch` = any |
| `SetLineWeight` | `set-line-weight` (new, B-1) | `branch` |
| `MarkServed`, `ReleaseItems` | `mark-served` | dine-in: the current table (`own_tables` = holder, 01b :194; `my_floors`; `branch`); takeaway / parking: as `RemoveItems` |
| `BoostOrder` | `boost-order` | as `MarkServed` |
| `RequestUnpaidHandover` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-6)) | `mark-served` | as `MarkServed` (takeaway / parking: as `RemoveItems`); the answer needs `hand-over-unpaid` (`branch` only, 01b v1.12 B-5; listed devices only, 05a v1.0.7 L-1), §6.12a |
| `ReturnToKitchen` | `mark-item-returned` | as `MarkServed` |
| `CompLine` | `apply-comp` (approvable) | `branch` |
| `AddZoneStock`, `SetZoneStock` | `manage-zone-stock` (new, B-2) | `my_zones` (B-3) or `branch` |
| query `order-contact` | **`view-customer-contact`** (new, B-5) | as `MarkServed` |
| query `customer-orders` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT)) | `view-customer-contact` | `branch` = every matching order; `my_floors` and `own_tables` = only matching orders **the caller placed** (O10, B-4: a phone exists only on takeaway / parking orders, which belong to their placer); filtered inside the query's statement (§6.18b) |
| query `top-items` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R9, G-15, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT)) | none (any staff session) | none (item counts are not secret, as `zone-stock`) |

B-4 adds `own_tables` to the scope sets of `take-order`, `boost-order`, `mark-item-returned` (01b `PERMISSION_SCOPES`) as an **option**. Defaults by `default_key` (README :185, R29 re-seed) keep **03a AD-32**: `mark-served` at `branch` for everyone who has it. Owner, manager: all, `branch`; cashier: keeps `take-order` (`branch`, 01b v1.6 :966) and adds `set-line-weight`, `apply-comp` (approval by manager), **`mark-served` (`branch`)** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-23): the kiosk serves its counter takeaways; not `boost-order`; 01b v1.8.1; was: no `mark-served`); order_taker: `take-order` (`branch`, as 01b v1.6 :967), `mark-served`, `mark-item-returned`, `boost-order` (all `branch`), `apply-comp` (approval by manager); waiter: `mark-served` (`branch`); **`manage-zone-stock`: owner and manager only by default** — the restaurant grants it (scope `my_zones` or `branch`) to whoever refills, a floor manager or a dedicated person (owner Q5 (b)), so the low / out alerts reach only them and managers; `view-customer-contact`: owner, manager, cashier at `branch`; order_taker at `own_tables` (orders they placed and tables they hold); not waiter. A waiter never holds a seating, so no waiter default uses `own_tables`. All editable (config).

## §5. Data model (hub SQLite)

Migration `apps/hub/src/db/migrations/NNNN_orders.sql`, next free number at build time.

```sql
CREATE TABLE orders (
  order_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_type      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (order_type IN ('dine_in','takeaway','parking')),
  table_key       TEXT NOT NULL,
  business_date   TEXT NOT NULL,
  token           INTEGER,                               -- takeaway only
  car_number      TEXT, car_type TEXT CHECK (car_type IN ('sedan','mini','large')),
  customer_name   TEXT, phone TEXT,                      -- OrderContactSet (O17)
  order_note      TEXT,
  placed_by       TEXT NOT NULL,                         -- envelope actor_id
  placed_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,                      -- recorded_at
  follow_up       INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (follow_up IN (0,1)),
  cancelled_at    INTEGER,
  billed_at       INTEGER,                               -- OrdersBilled (every bill, §3.3); ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-2): cleared by OrdersUnbilled
  day_closed_at   INTEGER,                               -- BusinessDayClosed: cancelled, billed or finished orders, not carried ones (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-5)); cleared by OrdersUnbilled (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-2))
  sent_after_reconnect INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (sent_after_reconnect IN (0,1)),
  bill_key        TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): takeaway / parking only (NULL for dine-in, §3.1); folded from OrderPlaced only (migration 0021_orders_billing)
  carried_over    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (carried_over IN (0,1)),  -- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-5): OrdersCarriedOver sets 1; applyDayClosed skips and clears it (migration 0021_orders_billing)
  pickup_at_ms    INTEGER,                               -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): OrderPlaced.pickup_at_ms (takeaway only, §3.2 step 2b); NULL = no pickup (migration NNNN_orders_takeaway)
  phone_key       TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): phoneKey(phone) in the OrderContactSet fold (§3.1); NULL when phone is NULL
  pickup_boosted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (pickup_boosted IN (0,1)), -- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1): set by OrderBoosted for a pickup order
  unpaid_handover_approved_at INTEGER,                   -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 owner review (OR-7): UnpaidHandoverApproved (§3.7a); NULL = no effective approval; in NNNN_orders_takeaway.sql (unmerged, so no new file); ~~never leaves the hub~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-m-12): the column is hub-only (a projection); the event syncs to the cloud like every order event (migration NNNN_orders_takeaway) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8)
  unpaid_handover_approved_due_minor INTEGER             -- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): nullable approved amount for this order; cleared with approved_at
);
-- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): was CREATE UNIQUE INDEX orders_token ON orders (business_date, token) WHERE token IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX orders_token_day ON orders (business_date, token) WHERE token IS NOT NULL;   -- token MAX; NOT unique: an addition keeps its root's token (§3.11); replaces orders_token in 0021
CREATE INDEX orders_bill_key ON orders (bill_key);                                           -- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): 05 bill lookup (0021)
CREATE INDEX orders_open ON orders (order_type, placed_at)
  WHERE billed_at IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL AND day_closed_at IS NULL;              -- open-orders, catch-up
-- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R3, G-1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): paid takeaways not yet handed over (§3.3 Open (lists)); the second branch of openOrderRows (NNNN)
CREATE INDEX orders_open_takeaway_paid ON orders (placed_at)
  WHERE order_type = 'takeaway' AND billed_at IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL AND day_closed_at IS NULL;
-- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): customer-orders (§6.18b) (NNNN)
CREATE INDEX orders_phone ON orders (phone_key, placed_at) WHERE phone_key IS NOT NULL;
-- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R9, G-15, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): top-items window (§6.18c) (NNNN)
CREATE INDEX orders_placed ON orders (placed_at);

CREATE TABLE order_lines (
  order_item_id   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  order_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  parent_order_item_id TEXT,
  line_kind       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (line_kind IN ('item','option_child','deal','deal_part')),
  item_id TEXT, deal_id TEXT, name TEXT NOT NULL,
  sale_unit       TEXT NOT NULL,
  quantity        INTEGER,                               -- NULL while weight pending
  units           INTEGER NOT NULL,                      -- §3.4
  line_total_minor INTEGER,                              -- copy; LineWeightSet
  refundable_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,           -- §3.4: line total, or a deal part's allocated + options; 0 deal / return
  removed_units INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, removed_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  voided_units  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, refund_minor  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  comped_units  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, comp_minor    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  comp_voided_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,          -- O21
  returned_units INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,             -- engine view only; money-live (O20)
  return_of       TEXT,                                  -- return lines (money 0); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-15, owner 2026-11-11): folded from ItemAdded.return_of (was: no source event)
  part_of         TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.20 (owner 2026-11-22, STAB-5 phase 1): ItemAdded.part_of, the first part of a split line (§3.2 step 4a); NULL = not split. Hub migration 0026_order_line_part_of (ADD COLUMN, no refold)
  served_from_zone_id TEXT,
  added_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,                      -- zone undo window
  served_at       INTEGER,
  served_source   TEXT CHECK (served_source IN ('tap','estimated','zone')),
  is_food         INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (is_food IN (0,1)),   -- engine line, not zone-served, not ready item (§3.3)
  kitchen_note    TEXT,
  station_id      TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.12 (04 Q-14): ItemAdded.station_id; NULL for a deal header (migration 0019_order_line_station)
  option_quantity INTEGER,                               -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.13 (04 Q-19): ItemAdded.option_quantity; NULL on non-option_child lines and on a child added later (migration 0020_order_line_option_quantity)
  options_json    TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.7 (owner 2026-11-11, B3-2): ItemAdded.options as [{ name, quantity }]; NULL = none (migration 0016_order_line_options; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.9 (B3-5): backfilled by 0016's SQL UPDATE from the stored ItemAdded events)
  tax_profile_id  TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-1): ItemAdded.tax_class_id (the item's tax profile; a deal_part carries the deal's, never used: its base is 0); NULL = untaxed item (migration 0021_orders_billing)
  option_id       TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): ItemAdded.option_id (an option_child's option; NULL on other kinds) (NNNN)
  deal_part_id    TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): ItemAdded.deal_part_id (a deal_part's part; NULL on other kinds) (NNNN)
  option_refs_json TEXT,                                 -- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): ItemAdded.options as [{ option_id, quantity }] in payload order; NULL = none (NNNN); options_json unchanged
  unit_price_minor INTEGER,                              -- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (R-7, R-17): round_half_up(line_total_minor ÷ units), set where line_total_minor is set (ItemAdded, LineWeightSet); NULL while a weight is pending; 0 on a deal_part (migration 0021_orders_billing)
  CHECK (removed_units + voided_units <= units),
  CHECK (comped_units <= units - removed_units - voided_units),
  CHECK (comp_minor + comp_voided_minor + removed_minor + refund_minor <= refundable_minor)
);
CREATE INDEX order_lines_order ON order_lines (order_id);                                  -- every per-order read

CREATE TABLE zone_stock (
  zone_id TEXT NOT NULL, item_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  on_hand INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (on_hand >= 0),
  PRIMARY KEY (zone_id, item_id)
);
```

- **One projection `orders`** (with `reset`) writes these three tables and **reads only its own events**: `OrderPlaced`, `ItemAdded`, `OrderLinesAdded`, `ItemRemoved`, `ItemVoided`, `ItemReturned`, `ItemServed`, `LineWeightSet`, `LineComped`, `OrderContactSet`, `OrderCancelled`, `OrdersBilled`, ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-6): `UnpaidHandoverApproved`, `OpenOrderBillChanged`, `OrderBoosted`, ⚠️ Changed by 05 (rows Q-2, Q-4, Q-5): `OrdersUnbilled`, `LineCompRemoved`, `OrdersCarriedOver`, `BusinessDayClosed` (with the order's own lines and `billed_at`), `ZoneStockAdded`, `ZoneStockSet`. No fold reads another projection (02a T19); every bill reaches 02b as `OrdersBilled` (§3.3). Replay identity holds in any registration order (test 28). ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1)
- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.7 (owner 2026-11-11, B3-2): the column `order_lines.options_json` is added by hub migration **`0016_order_line_options.sql`** (`ALTER TABLE order_lines ADD COLUMN options_json TEXT` and `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'orders'`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.9 (owner 2026-11-11, B3-5): **no** checkpoint delete; the migration is `ALTER TABLE order_lines ADD COLUMN options_json TEXT` plus one backfill `UPDATE order_lines SET options_json = (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('name', json_extract(o.value, '$.name'), 'quantity', json_extract(o.value, '$.quantity')) ORDER BY o.key) FROM events e, json_each(e.payload, '$.options') o WHERE e.event_type = 'ItemAdded' AND json_extract(e.payload, '$.order_item_id') = order_lines.order_item_id) WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events e WHERE e.event_type = 'ItemAdded' AND json_extract(e.payload, '$.order_item_id') = order_lines.order_item_id AND json_array_length(e.payload, '$.options') > 0)` — the same JSON as the live fold (`[{ name, quantity }]` in payload order, compact like `JSON.stringify` — checked byte-equal on the bundled SQLite 3.53, which supports the aggregate `ORDER BY`; `NULL` when there are none). Reason: a `ClockAlarm` written at boot step 3a, before projection catch-up, recreates the `orders` checkpoint row, so a deleted checkpoint does not force the rebuild. The `orders` projection's checkpoint is never touched by a migration (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.12 (04 Q-14): superseded for `0019_order_line_station.sql` by 00 v1.10 H-2 (`applyAll` never inserts a checkpoint row); see the next bullet). The `orders` fold of `ItemAdded` writes `JSON([{ name, quantity }])` from `ItemAdded.options` (`NULL` for `[]`), in the same `INSERT` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.12 (04 Q-14): and `station_id` = `ItemAdded.station_id`, `NULL` for a deal header). ~~**Backfill:** with its checkpoint row gone, the projection registry's catch-up at the next boot treats `orders` as never run (`projections/registry.ts`: no `projection_checkpoints` row → `from = 0` and `reset` before the first event), so `orders.reset` empties its tables and the whole log is re-applied, filling `options_json` for every existing line; no data `UPDATE` in the migration.~~ (superseded by v1.0.9 B3-5: SQL backfill above.) 03b reads it (`buildLineDetails`, 03b v1.0.3 §4.4).
- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.12 (04 Q-14): the column `order_lines.station_id TEXT NULL` is added by hub migration **`0019_order_line_station.sql`** (the next free number on `module-04`): `ALTER TABLE order_lines ADD COLUMN station_id TEXT` and `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'orders'`. The `orders` fold of `ItemAdded` writes `station_id` from `ItemAdded.station_id` (`NULL` for a deal header; a routed line keeps the station it was routed to). **Refold:** with 00 v1.10 H-2 built, `applyAll` only updates existing checkpoint rows, so a boot-time `ClockAlarm` (step 3a) no longer recreates the deleted row; `catchUp` finds no row, runs `orders.reset` and replays the log from seq 0, filling `station_id` for every existing line (and rewriting `options_json` with the same JSON as 0016's backfill). B3-5's reason for an SQL backfill is gone after H-2, so no `UPDATE` is needed. Forward-only projections that read `order_lines` (03c `kitchen_tickets`) keep their checkpoints. Read by 04 §3.1 through `readOrder` (u10). **0019 must not land on any branch without 00 v1.10 H-2 (ships only with `module-04`).**
- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.13 (owner 2026-11-12, 04 Q-19): the column `order_lines.option_quantity INTEGER NULL` is added by hub migration **`0020_order_line_option_quantity.sql`** (next free after `0019_order_line_station.sql`): `ALTER TABLE order_lines ADD COLUMN option_quantity INTEGER` and `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'orders'`. The `orders` fold of `ItemAdded` writes it from `ItemAdded.option_quantity`. **Refold** exactly as 0019 (00 v1.10 H-2: `catchUp` finds no row, `orders.reset`, replay from seq 0), filling `option_quantity` for every existing line; no `UPDATE`. Read by §3.4's cascade through `readOrder` and the comp line read. **0020 ships only with `module-04`** (as 0019).
- ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-1; with rows Q-5, Q-6): hub migration **`0021_orders_billing.sql`** (next free after `0020_order_line_option_quantity.sql`; the one 02b migration of 05) adds `order_lines.unit_price_minor INTEGER NULL` (⚠️ Changed by 05 (R-7, R-17), owner rulings of 2026-11-19: the `orders` fold writes `round_half_up(line_total_minor ÷ units)` — integer `floor((2 × line_total_minor + units) ÷ (2 × units))` — **where it writes `line_total_minor`**: the `ItemAdded` fold, and the `LineWeightSet` fold (a weighed line has `units` = 1, §3.4, so its unit price is its whole weighed `line_total_minor`); while a weight is pending the line has no total, so `unit_price_minor` is NULL; a `deal_part` (`line_total_minor` 0) = 0; an `option_child` = its own `line_total_minor ÷ units`; `item` and `deal` lines likewise from their own `line_total_minor` and `units`; `LineView.unit_price_minor` and `ReadOrderLine.unit_price_minor` read it, same statements, §4.2), `order_lines.tax_profile_id TEXT NULL` (the `orders` fold of `ItemAdded` writes it from `ItemAdded.tax_class_id`; the event field keeps its name; `LineView.tax_profile_id` and `ReadOrderLine.tax_profile_id` read it, same statements, §4.2), `orders.carried_over` (Q-5, §3.3) and `orders.bill_key` with the index `orders_bill_key` (Q-6, §3.1), and **replaces** the unique `orders_token` by the non-unique `orders_token_day (business_date, token)` (Q-6, §3.11), and runs `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'orders'` so `orders` refolds from seq 0 at the next boot (00 v1.10 H-2: `catchUp` finds no row, runs `orders.reset`, replays the log; the 0019 / 0020 pattern; no `UPDATE`). The hub migration tests that list or re-run the whole directory change with 0021 (`test/db/alerts-migration.int.test.ts`, `order-line-{options,station,option-quantity}-migration.int.test.ts`); `test/orders/migration.int.test.ts` stops at 0013 and is unchanged; a new 0021 test is added.
- ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK audit, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT) (R1, R3, R4, R9): hub migration **`NNNN_orders_takeaway.sql`** (next free contiguous hub number on `module-02b-tk` when its PR merges, D6): `ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN pickup_at_ms INTEGER`, `ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN pickup_boosted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (pickup_boosted IN (0,1))`, `ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN phone_key TEXT`, `ALTER TABLE order_lines ADD COLUMN option_id TEXT`, `… deal_part_id TEXT`, `… option_refs_json TEXT`, the indexes `orders_open_takeaway_paid`, `orders_phone`, `orders_placed` (SQL above), ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-6): `ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN unpaid_handover_approved_at INTEGER` and `… unpaid_handover_approved_due_minor INTEGER` (nullable; named-order fold §6.19), and `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'orders'` so `orders` refolds from seq 0 at the next boot (the 0019 / 0020 / 0021 pattern, 00 v1.10 H-2; no `UPDATE`). The folds: `OrderPlaced` writes `pickup_at_ms` (the event field, `null` on every order logged before v1.0.25); `OrderBoosted` sets `pickup_boosted = 1` for a pickup order without changing the time; `OrderContactSet` writes `phone_key = phone === null ? null : phoneKey(phone)` beside `customer_name` / `phone`; `ItemAdded` writes `option_id`, `deal_part_id` and `option_refs_json` (`JSON([{ option_id, quantity }])` from `ItemAdded.options`, `NULL` for `[]`) in the same `INSERT`. Replay identity (test 28) holds; forward-only projections that read `orders` / `order_lines` keep their checkpoints. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-1, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): `orders_open_takeaway_paid` holds every paid takeaway of the open day until day close, each probed by the `EXISTS`; the cost per call grows with the day inside one statement, so it is measured by `test:perf` (test 83); if it fails, a fold-maintained `orders.handed_at` replaces the `EXISTS` (a later bump). The hub migration tests that list or re-run the whole directory change with `NNNN`; a new `NNNN` test is added (test 65). ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-B-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 verify (R3-M-8) In the same migration add nullable INTEGER `orders.tracking_close_cutoff_ms` on the group root (initially NULL), rebuilt by the orders refold (§6.19).
- Times are `recorded_at`. No session ids.

## §6. Commands, events and queries

**Common rules.** `POST /commands/:name`, `sessionKind: 'device'`, staff session, D32 idempotency, no PIN. Responses `200 { data: { command_id, event_ids, result } }`; only `result` is listed. Dine-in **`SubmitOrder` and `RemoveItems`** carry `seating_id` (required) and follow 02a's `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` rule; other commands name orders or lines (run 4 m-2). Scope last (C8). Edits resolve the table by `currentTableOf`. Statement counts exclude 01d's own bounds and the append.

### 6.1 `SubmitOrder` (queueable)

| | |
|---|---|
| Permission | `take-order` |
| Payload | `{ order_id, order_type, table_id?, seating_id?, takeaway?: { customer_name?, phone?, print_slip?: boolean (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT), 03c row Q-TK-1: default false, root and addition, §3.2 steps 2b, 10a) }, adds_to_order_id?: string (takeaway only; ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6), §3.2 step 2), parking?: { car_number, car_type, phone? }, order_note?: ≤ 200, lines: [{ order_item_id, item_id? \| deal_id?, variant_id?, quantity?: int, options?: [{ option_id, quantity, order_item_id? }], deal_choices?: [{ deal_part_id, item_id, order_item_id, options? }], kitchen_note?: ≤ 80, from_zone?: boolean }] (1–40), pickup_at_ms?: int \| null (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): takeaway only, §3.2 step 2b) }`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-18, owner 2026-11-11): `variant_id` is accepted and ignored (01c V2: a variant is its own item) (was: listed with no meaning) |
| Scope | ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.5 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): dine-in: `requireLock` (holder, or `take-order-anywhere` covering the floor; 02a v1.1.4 §3.2, §4.2); no extra `my_floors` check on submit (§3.14) (was: "⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): dine-in `my_floors` on the named table, on every submit (§3.14) (was: no row)") |
| Script | §3.2 |
| Result | §3.2 step 11 (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-6): for a takeaway addition `token` = the `adds_to_order_id` order's token; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): + `slip`, 03c row Q-TK-3) |
| Statements | §3.2 (7 + append; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (c), `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): + ≤ 3 for a root / + ≤ 4 for an addition when `takeaway.print_slip` is true, 03c's `queueTakeawaySlipOnSubmit`, 0 without the flag, 03c row Q-TK-2). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): the zone path's count of 7 includes 01d's `listOpen` (§3.10), stricter than "exclude 01d's own bounds" (was: not stated) |

### 6.2 `RemoveItems`

`{ order_id, seating_id? (required for dine-in), lines: [{ order_item_id, units? }] (1–40, unique) }`; `take-order`; dine-in: `requireLock`. ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row T-3, R-39): on a `CLOSED` seating (`readOrder.seating_state`, a corrections-only reopen) the lock itself is skipped but the authorisation is not — see the statements below and 02a v1.1.10 §4.2. §3.5. Result `{ removed: [{ order_item_id, units }], cancelled: boolean, comp_requests_closed: alert_id[] }`. Statements: 1 order + lines, 1 seating / lock (dine-in; ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row T-3, R-39): on a `CLOSED` seating **no lock statement**, the authorisation is read as 02a v1.1.10 §4.2, no number stated here), 1 open comp requests (`alerts.listOpen` for the order), ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-1): +1 open `hand_over_unpaid` requests (`alerts.listOpen`, kind) only when the command cancels the order (§3.7a (b)), +1 zone alerts only when a zone line is in the payload (m-6), **+1 line guard when registered** (⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3; G-23); ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3): +1 per registered guard, §3.4). Errors: `ORDER_NOT_FOUND`, `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE`, `ORDER_BILLED`, lock errors, `VALIDATION_FAILED` (repeated entry, C6), `VOID_PENDING` (line guard, §3.4; ⚠️ Changed by 04, row Q-3), `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3)).

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3): On cancellation, close the pending named-order request `order_cancelled` as in §3.7a and §4.2. Use a single alert lookup for the affected order plus 01d’s close/result bound. In an approved void, 04 runs this close after the apply drafts fold, before commit.

### 6.3 `SetLineWeight`

`{ order_item_id, quantity }`; `set-line-weight`. §3.6. Result `{ line_total_minor }`. Statements: 1, **+1 line guard when registered** (⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3; G-23); ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3): +1 per registered guard, §3.4). Errors: `ORDER_NOT_FOUND`, `LINE_NOT_WEIGHED`, `LINE_ALREADY_SETTLED`, `ORDER_BILLED`, `VOID_PENDING` (⚠️ Changed by 04, row Q-3), `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3)).

### 6.4 `MarkServed` (queueable)

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): `{ lines: [{ order_id, order_item_ids }] (1–20 orders), customer_handover?: true }`; `mark-served`; no lock. Result `{ served: n, skipped: order_item_id[] }`. Omitted flag uses ordinary §3.7 serving. All staff takeaway/parking customer actions in §8 send `customer_handover: true`, including partial and queued actions. Explicit customer hand-over accepts takeaway/parking only (else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` with `issues[]` at `lines[i].order_id`).

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): In the writer transaction, resolve the actor’s current grant and scope, read the selected orders and their live lines, and perform the usual stage/skip checks. Compare the remaining unserved live non-deal IDs with the actual IDs to serve, using the combined selection per order. Take one fresh `keyBalances` read for all unbilled affected keys. For any Due order whose last live line would be served, require its own effective approval at the fresh due (D2/D3). A paid or nonpositive balance needs none. Refuse the whole send before appending with `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: "unpaid_handover_approval_required", order_id }` when it fails. Recheck on queued execution as on a live send; a previously processed command ID returns its stored result under D32.

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3): Base reads: 2–3 (orders incl. bill key and approval, lines, batched table scope only for dine-in). Explicit hand-over adds at most 1 batched due read. If a takeaway/parking order actually goes from having live unserved lines to none, do at most 1 batched pending-request read across all those orders and one `alerts.closeMany` for the hits (01d’s own close and result-raise cost). Ordinary errors `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` and `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE` stay as in §3.7.

### 6.4a `RequestUnpaidHandover` ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 owner review (OR-7)

`{ order_id }`; permission **`mark-served`**, order scope as `MarkServed` (§3.14: takeaway / parking as `RemoveItems`); staff session, `sessionKind: 'device'`; **not queueable** (a manager must answer now); idempotent by `command_id`.
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): **Script.** Read the named order (1); unknown → `404 ORDER_NOT_FOUND`. Wrong type → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { issues: [{ path: "order_id", message: "takeaway_or_parking_only" }] }`. Cancelled, day-closed or billed → `{ needed: false }`. Check scope. Read the named order’s key due with `keyBalances` (1, D3). Nonpositive due → `{ needed: false }`. An effective approval at this due → `{ approved: true }`. Otherwise look up the open request ~~for `dedupe_key = "hand_over_unpaid:" + order_id`~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9): **of the order's bill** — read the bill's live takeaway / parking orders by `bill_key` (1, oldest first), the subject is the oldest, `dedupe_key = "hand_over_unpaid:bill:" + bill_key` (1 targeted read). If its amount matches, return its `alert_id` and whether this caller is its original requester. If stale, append its `obsolete` close with note `due_changed` before the replacement raise so the same key can be used. Raise via `alerts.raise` with `kind: "hand_over_unpaid"`, subject `{ stream_type: "order", stream_id: order_id }`, target `{ kind: "answerers" }`, `values: { order_id, token, due_minor }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (Q9): `order_id` = the bill's subject order, plus `order_ids` = every live order of the bill), `details: { identifier, amount: due_minor }`, `display: { kind_label_key: "alert.hand_over_unpaid.lock" }` and the caller’s `requester_staff_id`. Identifier is `#<token>` or `PARKING`.
- ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2): **Result:** `{ needed: false } | { approved: true } | { alert_id, is_requester: boolean }`. **Reads:** 2 plus at most 1 targeted pending read, 01d’s raise bound on a new request (≤ 6 + R + T), and 01d’s close/result bound on replacement. An identical request incurs no new alert or result.
- **Errors:** `400`, `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`, `404 ORDER_NOT_FOUND`; ~~`409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-5): no day step; a day-closed order is `{ needed: false }`); no new code.

`{ order_ids, released, expedite }` (sets, C1); `mark-served`. §3.8. Result `{ alerts_closed: n }`. Statements: 1–2 (orders + lines, `currentTableOf` for dine-in scope) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): 1–2 as built; was: 2–3). Errors: `ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE`.

### 6.6 `BoostOrder`

`{ order_id }`; `boost-order`. Statements: 1–2 (order, scope) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-36, owner 2026-11-11): 1–2 as built; was: 2–3). Errors: `ORDER_NOT_FOUND`, `ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE`.

### 6.7 `ReturnToKitchen`

`{ order_item_id, return_order_item_id, mode }` (03a :500; device-minted id, O3); `mark-item-returned`. §3.8. Result `{ eta_ms, promise_ms }`. Statements: 2–3 (line, `return_order_item_id` id check, scope). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.8 (owner 2026-11-11, B3-4): 3–4 — + 1 read of the served line's option children (`order_lines WHERE parent_order_item_id = ?`) for the copied options. Errors: `ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE`, `RETURN_ALREADY_OPEN`, `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE`, `ORDER_ITEM_ID_IN_USE`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-31–AD-35, owner 2026-11-11): the list gains `409 STATION_PAUSED` (the line's station is paused and has no allowed alternate, AD-31); `404 MENU_ITEM_NOT_FOUND { item_id }` (`fix` when the item is no longer in the config, so there is no `adjust_time_ms`, AD-32); `404 ORDER_NOT_FOUND { order_item_id }` (unknown `order_item_id`, AD-33, as §9); and `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE` stages `'zone_served'` (a zone-served line, AD-34) and `'not_engine_line'` (a deal header, or a line the engine does not hold, on an order still in the engine, AD-35; a distinct word, not `'out_of_engine'`) (was: the four codes above only).

### 6.8 `CompLine`

`{ order_item_id, units, reason_code, note?: ≤ 120 }`; `apply-comp`, `approvable: true`; online only. §3.9. Result `{ comped: true, value_minor } | { alert_id, pending: true, value_minor }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-11): `value_minor` on the pending result; was `{ alert_id, pending: true }`). Statements: 2 (line + children, open comp requests of the order) + 01d, **+1 line guard when registered** (⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-3; G-12, G-23): asked with the plan's ids, before scope, every grant; ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3): +1 per registered guard, §3.4). Errors: `ORDER_NOT_FOUND`, `COMP_NOT_ALLOWED`, `COMP_PENDING`, `LINE_WEIGHT_PENDING`, `ORDER_BILLED`, `VOID_PENDING` (⚠️ Changed by 04, row Q-3), `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3)).

### 6.9 `AddZoneStock` / 6.10 `SetZoneStock`

§3.10. `manage-zone-stock`, scope `my_zones` on the zone. Results `{ on_hand }`. Statements: 2 (count, open low / out alerts via `alerts.listOpen` for subject `zone:<id>`) + the branch stock read on refill from the engine state in memory (0 SQL) + 01d (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-25, owner 2026-11-11): `dayDraws` / `saleUnitsAvailable` on `ctx.state`; was: "1 branch stock read"). Errors: `ZONE_NOT_FOUND`, `ZONE_ITEM_NOT_CONFIGURED`, `STOCK_INSUFFICIENT`, `BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN` (refill).

Registered with 03b's `Handover` (O14, §3.7); text kept for then.

### 6.12 Kind `comp`

```typescript
defineAlertKind({
  kind: 'comp', family: 'action', subject: 'order',
  steps: APPROVAL_TIER_STEPS,
  closes_on: 'owner',                          // 02b closes stale ones 'obsolete' (§3.9); 05 at day close
  values_schema: z.object({ order_id: z.string().min(1), order_item_id: z.string().min(1),
                            units: z.number().int().min(1), reason_code: z.string().min(1),
                            note: z.string().max(120).nullable() }),   // E0 AC3: 'other' keeps its note
  details_schema: AlertDetailsBase.pick({ item: true, quantity: true, amount: true, reason: true }),
  lead_fallback: false, expires_at_day_close: false, silent: false,
  labels: { card: 'alert.comp.card', lock: 'alert.comp.lock' },
  request: {
    answers: ['approve', 'reject'],
    right: { mode: 'approval', base_permission: 'apply-comp' },
    needs_pin: 'always',
    apply(ctx, answer),          // approve: re-check §3.9 → plan → line guard (⚠️ Changed by 04, row Q-3: 409 VOID_PENDING; 1 → 2 statements when 04's guard is registered; ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-3): +1 for 05's, §3.4) → [LineComped { …, alert_id, approver_staff_id }]; reject → []
    onWithdraw: () => [],
  },
});
```

⚠️ Changed by 01d v1.3 (L-4b): **Target** — `link: (v) => ({ type: 'order_line', order_id: v.order_id, order_item_id: v.order_item_id })`. A tap opens the line (table sheet round or `#/orders/<id>`), unfolding a served round. It shows "This item is no longer open." when the order is cancelled or billed or the line has no live units (01d v1.3 §3.13.1 row 2). Its `request_result` links the same line (row 14).

### 6.12a Kind `hand_over_unpaid` ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 owner review (OR-7)

Registered by 02b ~~(`apps/hub/src/orders/kinds.ts`)~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-m-8): `apps/hub/src/orders/hand-over-unpaid-kind.ts`, imported statically before the alerts replay, as `comp-kind.ts`; 01d P-8) through 01d `defineAlertKind`:

⚠️ Changed by run 3 reconcile (AlertRaised v6): `AlertRaised` v6 is **defined once in 01d v1.3.7 §5.4** (the permission target arm, `request.base_permission` and `request.range_permission` all frozen at v1–v5); this paragraph only says how 02b uses it. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-B-1): Register `AlertRaised` **v6** in the pilot slice before OR-7 is enabled. The holders right stores `hand-over-unpaid` in `request.base_permission`. Freeze the v1–v5 persisted permission enums, including prior persisted target branches using the shared enum, and use the widened schema in v6. Register the identity v5→v6 upcaster (Foundation D46/D5); old raise fixtures replay unchanged and the new request appends and replays as v6. 01b B-5 and 01d use this same version.

```ts
defineAlertKind({
  kind: 'hand_over_unpaid', family: 'urgent', subject: 'order',          // { stream_type: 'order', stream_id: order_id }
  // ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): TK-Q21 accepted; urgent has a request part.
  steps: [{ at: { ms: 0 }, to: [{ target: { kind: 'answerers' }, set: 'answerer' }] }],
  closes_on: 'owner', expires_at_day_close: true, silent: false, lead_fallback: false,
  // ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-B-1): no skip_requester (01d kinds.ts:160 refuses it on a request kind; requests already skip the requester, 01d §3.3 rule 5)
  // expires_at_day_close: true has no effect on a request kind (01d kinds.ts:163 needs it for closes_on 'owner'); 02b / 05 close the request (§3.7a, R2-M-1)
  values_schema: z.object({ order_id: z.string().min(1),
                            // ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2): one named order per request
                            token: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),          // ⚠️ hostile run 2 (R2-M-2): null for parking
                            due_minor: z.number().int().positive(),                // the script never raises with due_minor ≤ 0 (§6.4a)
                            // ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9): every live order of the request's bill (absent on an older alert = [order_id])
                            order_ids: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1).optional() }),
  details_schema: AlertDetailsBase.pick({ identifier: true, amount: true, disposition: true }), // identifier '#<token>' or 'PARKING' (≤ 40); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q10): disposition 'alert.hand_over_unpaid.addition' for an addition-only bill
  labels: { card: "alert.hand_over_unpaid.card", lock: "alert.hand_over_unpaid.lock" }, // ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-4)
  request: {
    answers: ['approve', 'reject'],
    right: { mode: 'holders', permission: 'hand-over-unpaid' },          // 01d §3.3 holders right (05 row D-1 pattern)
    needs_pin: 'always',                                                  // money: food leaves without payment
    apply(ctx, answer) {                                                  // read-only: returns drafts (01d read-only contract)
      // ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): approve rechecks named order and fresh due (see below).
      // → [UnpaidHandoverApproved { order_id, alert_id, approved_by_staff_id: answer.answerer_staff_id, due_minor: fresh_due_minor }]
      // reject: → []
    },
  },
});
```

~~The answerers are the holders of `hand-over-unpaid` with a scope covering the order (`branch` by default)~~ ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-m-6): the answerers are the holders of `hand-over-unpaid` (`branch` only, 01b v1.12 B-5), as 01d's holders right resolves them, presence-filtered (01d §3.4.1), the requester excluded (a requesting manager cannot answer: TK-Q20); on an `everyone` device 05a's listed-only mask makes the answer `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'listed_device_only' }` (01d §3.3 rule 2a, before argon2) and 01d shows "Approve on a listed device" (05a §8). ~~No approver at all → 01d's existing `no_approver` notice.~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-m-7): `hand-over-unpaid` is allowed-only, so 01d's `no_approver` (§3.9, `requires_approval` permissions only) never fires for it; with no holder present the requester sees "Waiting — find a manager", 01d §6.8.) Audit (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-m-5)): `UnpaidHandoverApproved` maps to the 04 audit action `unpaid_handover_approved` (`registerAuditAction`), the request and its answer to `unpaid_handover_requested` / `unpaid_handover_answered` (`registerRequestAction('hand_over_unpaid', …)`); the three actions join 04's closed `AUDIT_ACTIONS` list (row TK-16).

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): On approve, the read-only apply runs in 01d pre-read and again in the writer transaction after the ordinary current permission/listed-device checks. Each pass reads the named order (1) and, if still eligible, its fresh D3 due (1). Append an approval only for a live, unbilled order with positive due equal to the card amount. A committed obsolete close is handled by 01d’s normal `REQUEST_NOT_PENDING` path. If a stale card remains open at apply, refuse `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: "alert_id", reason: "unpaid_handover_request_stale" }`, no answer or approval write; the client refetches and asks again, which replaces the stale card. Decline needs no balance read.

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-4): Use a static audit-map import before the audit projection seals the registry (as `billing/index.ts` imports its map). The lock string is “Unpaid hand-over request” with no placeholders. The signed-in card is “Hand over unpaid {identifier}”, with the identifier and amount from signed-in details; localize both.

### 6.13 Kinds `zone_stock_low`, `zone_stock_out`

```typescript
defineAlertKind({
  kind: 'zone_stock_low',                      // 'zone_stock_out': same shape, card 'alert.zone_stock_out.card'

                               { target: { kind: 'managers' }, set: 'recipient' }] }],  // Z7 + owner Q5 (b)
  closes_on: 'owner',                          // 02b closes 'done' when the count goes back above threshold
  values_schema: z.object({ zone_id: z.string().min(1), item_id: z.string().min(1),
                            on_hand: z.number().int().min(0), threshold: z.number().int().min(0) }),
```
