The banner and Inbox tap open `#/zones?focus=item:<zone_id>:<item_id>` and glow the row (01d v1.3 §3.13.1 rows 5, 6). This replaces the banner's plain `navigate('/zones')`.

### 6.14 `GET /queries/eta-preview`

⚠️ Changed by v1.0.26 (owner 2026-11-28, CT-4): every `lines[]` entry also carries `ready_ms` = `eta_ms` + `paddingOf(state, the line's planned station, order_type)` (null when `eta_ms` is null), the line's customer time (CT-3); additive, 0 statements.

Staff session; `countsAsActivity: false`. Query `{ draft }` = JSON of a `SubmitOrder` payload without ids (temp `key`s), ≤ 8 KB, Zod-parsed (over → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`). Runs §3.2 steps 4–8 read-only (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK audit, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): **step 1 first** — no open day → `409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, 0 statements, nothing appended; as built, `eta-preview.ts:108-109`, test `eta-preview.int.test.ts:285`; was: steps 4–8 only, no day check stated; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): and step 2b on the draft's `pickup_at_ms` (takeaway only, never queued, so `t0 = now`) → `422 PICKUP_TIME_INVALID` / `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` for the whole preview, and the draft's checked pickup goes into `proposed.order.pickup_at_ms`, so the preview's `promise_ms` is the pickup's; the draft schema gains `pickup_at_ms?: int | null`; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-8, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): for a takeaway draft the result gains **`pickup_bounds: { earliest_ms, latest_ms }`** (step 2b's numbers with `t0 = now`, also when the draft has no pickup; `null` for other types), so the picker uses the hub's clock; 0 statements), with `follow_up` read as in §3.3: `reserveStock` (pure, nothing appended) then `calculateETA(state, proposed, now, from_stock)`. Result `{ as_of_seq, groups: [{ promise_ms, keys }], lines: [{ key, eta_ms, from_stock, zone_served, refused?: { code, details } }] }`. Statements: 3 (availability, zone counts, `follow_up`) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK audit, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): 0 when no day is open). p99 < 40 ms at 12 lines. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-26, owner 2026-11-11): the draft travels in the GET query string, and URL-encoded (Urdu notes grow about 6×) it can pass Node's default 16 KB `maxHeaderSize`, which would answer `431` before the 8 KB check; the hub's `maxHeaderSize` is **64 KB** (a 00 platform knob in `server.ts`, for every route), so an over-size draft gets the `400` above (was: not stated).

### 6.15 `GET /queries/seating-orders`

`{ seating_id }` → the seating's orders (current, P-1c), by `placed_at`: `{ order_id, placed_by: { staff_id, name }, placed_at, follow_up, cancelled, billed, has_phone, lines: LineView + stage (from `EngineState`), groups: [{ promise_ms }] }`, `total_minor`. Statements: 2. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-37, owner 2026-11-11): each order carries one order-level `promise_ms` (`EngineOrder.promise_ms`) instead of `groups: [{ promise_ms }]`; `EngineState` holds no per-group promise (was: `groups: [{ promise_ms }]`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R8, owner 2026-11-11): every line also carries `fired: boolean` from the engine state (0 statements), so the client can enable Mark served (§3.7) (was: no `fired`). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-7): each order carries `order_note`, each line its `options` (`LineView`, §4.2; 0 statements) (was: neither). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-11): each line carries `pending_comp: { alert_id, units, value_minor } | null` from the open `comp` requests of the seating's orders (one `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'comp' })` per call, counted here); **Statements: 3** (was: 2, no pending comp).

### 6.16 `GET /queries/open-orders`

`{ order_type?: 'takeaway' | 'parking' | 'all', abandoned?: boolean }` → open non-table orders (`orders_open`; token / car, `has_phone`, ready n / m, promise, "from <date>"), by promise; `abandoned: true` (managers) adds orders of any type with no kitchen tap for `order_abandon_ms` (03a). Statements: 2. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R1, R2, R12, owner 2026-11-11): `abandoned=true` is for managers only (R1); `abandoned` with `order_type` still lists every type (R12); an order with **no engine lines** (only no-station, ready or zone-served lines) is **never** in the abandoned list, since it is not waiting on the kitchen (R2) (was: no rule for an order with no engine lines). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R3, owner 2026-11-11): in ready n / m, deal headers are left out and return lines count in m; a dine-in line is ready when it is served or at engine stage `done` / `served`; for takeaway/parking, count served lines and the readiness of each live unserved line using §4.2’s rule. An older Pass mark cannot count a line that is still to make. A no-station line counts only once its effective pickup hold has ended (was: not defined). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R4, owner 2026-11-11): the promise = `EngineOrder.promise_ms` while the order is in the engine, else null; null sorts last (was: "by promise", no rule for an order out of the engine). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R5, owner 2026-11-11): "from <date>" (`from_date`) is set when the order's `business_date` ≠ the open day's date; while no day is open, that is every order (was: not defined). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-9): each row carries `placed_by: { staff_id, name }` (name from the in-memory staff directory, 0 statements) and, for a dine-in row (abandoned list), `table_id` = the seating's current primary table (one batched read, only when dine-in rows are listed); **Statements: ≤ 3** (was: 2, no `placed_by` / `table_id`). The customer name stays in the catch-up / `OrderContactSet` (O17); the abandon text uses `order_abandon_ms` ("No kitchen tap for 2 h+"). ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1)

⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R3, G-1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT). **Takeaway until handed over.** Which orders: §3.3 **Open (lists)** — a takeaway stays listed while it is not billed **or** has a live line not yet served; dine-in (abandoned list) and parking rows unchanged. `openOrderRows(db, types)` is **one** statement of two branches:

```sql
SELECT <cols> FROM orders o                                    -- orders_open (unchanged branch)
 WHERE o.billed_at IS NULL AND o.cancelled_at IS NULL AND o.day_closed_at IS NULL
   AND (? = 1 OR o.order_type IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?)))
UNION ALL
SELECT <cols> FROM orders o                                    -- orders_open_takeaway_paid
 WHERE o.order_type = 'takeaway' AND o.billed_at IS NOT NULL AND o.cancelled_at IS NULL AND o.day_closed_at IS NULL
   AND (? = 1 OR 'takeaway' IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?)))
   AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM order_lines l WHERE l.order_id = o.order_id AND l.line_kind <> 'deal'
                 AND l.served_at IS NULL AND l.units - l.removed_units - l.voided_units > 0)
 ORDER BY placed_at, order_id
```

`<cols>` = today's columns plus `billed_at`, `bill_key`, `pickup_at_ms`, `pickup_boosted` and the two approval columns (the contact columns stay in the row for the catch-up only; the query body never carries them). New index `orders_open_takeaway_paid` (§5); `orders_open` unchanged. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8)

**Row fields added** (every row; 0 statements unless stated):

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| `bill` | `{ state: 'due' \| 'paid', due_minor }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.27 (owner 2026-11-28 Q9): plus `key` — the row's `bill_key` while `due`, `null` when `paid`; additive, so the client groups a token's orders by bill and asks once per bill) for takeaway and parking rows, `null` for dine-in rows. `paid` when the order's `billed_at` is set (`due_minor: 0`); else `due` with `due_minor` = 05's `keyBalances` for the row's `bill_key` (§4.1, row TK-5; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (B-1, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): `keyBalances(…).get(bill_key).due_minor`, 05 v1.0.35 §6.2a, **1 statement** for every unbilled row's key together, 0 when there is none). A takeaway addition after its root's bill closed reads `due` with the new bill's balance while the root reads `paid` (05 #44e). `due` with `due_minor: 0` is possible (everything comped, bill not closed); the client greys Hand over only when `due_minor > 0` (§8). Due uses §4.1’s sum over open split balances (D3) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) |
| `ready_at` | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1): Counter whole-bag Ready time from 03b’s batched `passReadyTimesOf` (one read, zero with no row). For takeaway/parking, use the completion that made every live line ready. Undo, return or remake at any point that makes the bag incomplete clears the counter Ready alert and `ready_at`. 03b observes the new whole-order completion, including after a pickup hold ends, and starts the counter alert and clock from the new time. Return the epoch as `ready_at` and its frozen `bag_ready_window_ms` (both null without an epoch). Both screens turn amber at `ready_at + bag_ready_window_ms` (D8), never on a partial bag. A Pass→counter hand-off alone does not clear a still-complete bag. Preserve the existing dine-in meaning. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 verify (R3-M-9) |
| `handed` | `{ n, m }`: m = the order's live lines (as ready n / m: no `deal` header, `live_units > 0`, return lines counted), n = those handed over (`served_at` set, §3.3). Split parts (`part_of`), option children and deal parts count as lines, as in ready n / m (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-5, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): so the client's Hand over sends **every** live unserved non-deal line id, §8; a display may count root lines instead) |
| `lines_summary` | `[{ name, qty }]`, at most **6**: the order's live root lines (`item` and `deal`, no option children, deal parts or return lines), in `added_at` order, merged by `part_of` (a split line is one entry) and then by equal `name`; `qty` = Σ live units (a weighed line counts 1) |
| `lines_more` | the number of summary entries left out after the 6 (0 when none) |
| `pickup_at_ms` / `effective_pickup_at_ms` | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1): Retained pickup from `orders.pickup_at_ms` and effective scheduling pickup as in §4.2. The latter drives “Phone orders — later”; once elapsed or boosted, use the now queue and the real promise. Hub sort remains by promise. |
| `planned_ready_at_ms` | ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling BR-1 (2026-11-27, L-CLIENT-5): the engine's current whole-order planned ready time for an open takeaway / parking order (the latest planned land of its live lines, held lines included, read from the in-memory engine state: 0 extra statements), `null` when every live line is already ready or the order is not in the engine. The client's token view, queue row and ready tile show it as **"Ready ~{planned}"** with the stored promise struck through beside it when the order is **boosted** and the planned time is earlier than the promise; otherwise they keep showing the promise. The stored promise itself never changes (03a PK-5). Also on the catch-up part `orders` (§6.18). ⚠️ BR-1 build (2026-11-27): the time is the **customer** time — the planned land plus the padding, by 03c §4.2 `customerReadyAt` for a boosted order — so the counter matches the receipt; dine-in rows carry `null`; a token shows "Ready ~" only when every not-ready order of it is boosted and planned, with the latest of them; a carried order still in the engine keeps its planned time. |

**No contact fields** (R3): no `customer_name`, no phone digits, no `phone_last4` on this query for any caller or device; queue search by token, name or last 4 digits runs on the client over the catch-up part `orders`, where the kiosk and the placer already receive the contact (O17, §6.18, §8). `has_phone` stays.

**Statements: ≤ 5** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (B-1, M-2, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) — orders (1), lines (1), `keyBalances` (1, only with an unbilled takeaway / parking row), `passReadyTimesOf` (1, only with a row), `currentTableOf` (1, only with a dine-in row of the abandoned list) (was: ≤ 3). Fixed whatever the row count (test 80: 1 and 50 rows). The effective pickup and approval comparison are in memory. Errors unchanged (`PERMISSION_DENIED` for `abandoned` by a non-manager, `VALIDATION_FAILED`). Audiences: unchanged (a query; §8 also refetches on Ready changes and the contact-free `OpenOrderBillChanged` frame (§6.19)). ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 verify (R3-M-9)

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): **`unpaid_handover_approved: boolean`** is true only on the named open, unbilled takeaway/parking row with an effective approved time and amount and current positive due no higher than the stored amount. Read approval columns with the existing order read, and compare against the already batched current due. Dine-in emits false. §6.19 clears approval when required and §6.4 checks again during hand-over.

### 6.17 `GET /queries/zone-stock`

`{ zone_id? }` → `[{ zone_id, name, items: [{ item_id, name, on_hand, threshold }] }]` — **any staff session** reads every zone's counts (order takers' tiles, the refill person's Count screen; W-3); `manage-zone-stock` and its scope gate only `AddZoneStock` / `SetZoneStock` (run 4 M-3). Statements: 1. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R11, owner 2026-11-11): an unknown or archived `zone_id` → `404 ZONE_NOT_FOUND`; rows whose item is no longer in the config are hidden (was: not stated).

### 6.18 `GET /queries/order-contact`

`{ order_id }`; `view-customer-contact` with the order's scope; `countsAsActivity: true` (a deliberate tap). → `{ customer_name, phone }`. Logged `info { order_id, staff_id }` (no values). Statements: 1, or 2 with scope `my_floors` on a dine-in order (the shared scope check reads `currentTableOf`) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-38, owner 2026-11-11); was: 1). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R9, owner 2026-11-11): the checks run in this order: permission → order → scope (was: no order stated).

### 6.18a `GET /queries/order-view`

⚠️ Added by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-8). `{ order_id }` → one order in the §6.15 `SeatingOrder` shape (same body builder: `placed_by`, `order_note`, lines with `stage`, `fired`, `options`, `pending_comp`, `promise_ms`), for the takeaway / parking token view (Mark served per line, Remove, Boost). Staff session; `countsAsActivity: false`. Scope for a takeaway / parking order: as `MarkServed` (§3.14, from `placed_by`; 0 statements); a dine-in order: any staff session, as `seating-orders` (⚠️ owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-27; §12.3 Q6). Unknown order → `404 ORDER_NOT_FOUND`. **Statements: 2** (order, lines) + the one `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'comp' })` of `pending_comp` (counted, as §6.15) = 3.

⚠️ Changed by 01d v1.3 (L-4b; hostile review B1). **The body also carries `order_type`** (`'dine_in' | 'takeaway' | 'parking'`, already read by the `ORDER` statement and dropped by `toSeatingOrder` until now) **and `seating_id`** (`string | null`): the seating the order is in **now**, from `seating_orders.seating_id`. That is the row `TablesMerged` and `SeatingSplit` rewrite (02a §3.11, §3.12), so it follows every merge and split. It is null for takeaway / parking (no `seating_orders` row). Both are read in the **same** `ORDER` statement (`… FROM orders o LEFT JOIN seating_orders so ON so.order_id = o.order_id WHERE o.order_id = ?`), so the count stays **3**. `orders.table_key` is not exposed: it is written at placement and is stale after a merge or split. Readers needing the seating use this field (01d v1.3 §3.13.3). Scope rules unchanged. Test 52. Until it lands the client ships the designed Served / Not served fallback (the hub's answers decide).

**Catch-up part `orders`:** (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R3, G-1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): "open" = §3.3 **Open (lists)**: the part reads the same `openOrderRows(db, 'all')` as `open-orders`, so a paid takeaway that is not yet handed over is in it — the kiosk's queue search (R3) needs it; each order carries its real `billed` / `billed_at` (was: `false` / `null` for every row) and `pickup_at_ms`; it does **not** carry `bill`, `ready_at`, `handed` or `lines_summary` (the queue reads them from `open-orders`); statements unchanged (4); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-4, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): **`billed` on catch-up rows is no longer constant**; the client keeps its seeded copy current by the §8 fold rule; was: not billed, not cancelled, not day-closed) all open orders of the branch (one statement) and their lines (one statement); the builder runs per socket (`build(db, state, socket)`) and includes `customer_name` / `phone` only for cashier kiosks and the placer's sockets (O17); plus `zone_stock` (one statement). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R10, owner 2026-11-11): the part goes to every staff socket and cashier kiosk, with every order type and no `stage` (was: not stated). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-10): the part gains `open_slips: [{ alert_id, order_ids }]` — the branch's open `slip_warning` alerts (one `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'slip_warning' })`), so the client groups late cards by current table and sends one `ReleaseItems` (§8); 03a unchanged (was: no `open_slips`).

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-7): The authorized `orders` catch-up remains the source of contact. Clients use the open IDs from §3.3 for membership, including day-close removal and reopen re-entry (§8). A refreshed catch-up replaces the device’s contact copy.

### 6.18b `GET /queries/customer-orders`

⚠️ Added by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT). The phone match and **Repeat last order** (TK-D01 PhoneRepeat).

| | |
|---|---|
| Query | `{ phone }`: the typed phone, sent with `encodeURIComponent`, validated as `SubmitOrder`'s (§3.1: `^\+?[\d\s-]+$`, 10–13 digits) → else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (`issues: [{ path: 'phone', … }]`; the issue never echoes the value) |
| Permission | `view-customer-contact` (01b B-5) → else `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`. Checked first (as §6.18: permission → query) |
| Scope | `branch`: every matching order. `my_floors`, `own_tables`: only matching orders **placed by the caller** (O10, B-4: a phone is only ever on takeaway / parking orders, which belong to their placer); the filter `o.placed_by = ?` is in statement 1, so 0 extra statements. Default holders (§3.14): owner, manager, cashier at `branch`; order_taker `own_tables`; not waiter |
| Session | staff session, `sessionKind: 'device'`; **`countsAsActivity: true`** (a deliberate lookup, as `order-contact`) |
| Match | `phone_key = phoneKey(phone)` (§3.1), over orders that are **not cancelled**, of any date and type (takeaway, parking), index `orders_phone`. A **visit** = the orders of one `bill_key` (a takeaway root and its additions; a parking plate's day), else the order alone: `visit = COALESCE(bill_key, order_id)`. An addition without its own contact has no `phone_key` and is reached through its visit |
| Statement 1 | over the matching orders: `name` = the newest non-null `customer_name`; `orders_count` = `COUNT(DISTINCT visit)`; the newest **3 visits** by their latest `placed_at` (`visit`, `placed_at` = its latest, `order_type`) |
| Statement 2 | only when statement 1 found a visit: the lines of every not-cancelled order of those ≤ 3 visits (`orders o JOIN order_lines l`, `o.bill_key IN (…) OR o.order_id IN (…)`; indexes `orders_bill_key`, `order_lines_order`); the visit's root `order_id` = its **earliest order over all orders of the `bill_key`**, matched or not (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-3, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT); was: the earliest matching order) |
| Result | `{ name: string \| null, orders_count, last: [{ order_id, placed_at, order_type, lines: RepeatLine[] }] }` newest visit first; no match → `{ name: null, orders_count: 0, last: [] }` (200). **No phone in the body** (the caller typed it) |
| `RepeatLine` | the `SubmitOrder` line shape without ids, built in memory from the visit's lines: root lines only (`item`, `deal`; no return lines), merged by `part_of` (a split line is one line again) and with live units only (`quantity` = Σ live units; a line with none is dropped; a weighed line → `quantity: null`, weigh again); `{ item_id? \| deal_id?, quantity, options: [{ option_id, quantity }], deal_choices?: [{ deal_part_id, item_id, options }], on_menu: boolean }`. `options` = the line's plain options (`option_refs_json`) and its live option children (`option_id`, `option_quantity`; a child with `option_quantity: null`, added later, is left out). `deal_choices` from the deal's parts (`deal_part_id`, `item_id`, their options the same way). **`on_menu: false`** when the item, deal, a chosen deal part, a deal part's item or an option is no longer in `branchConfig()` (archived or deleted), or (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-3, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) an option is not offered at this branch or no longer in one of the item's current modifier groups ( availability and hours are checked at Send, §3.2); the client skips those when repeating. No prices (the cart reprices from config, D25), no kitchen note |
| Statements | **2** (1 when nothing matches) |
| Log | `info { staff_id, matched: orders_count > 0 }` only (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-3, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT); was `{ staff_id }`) — never the phone, its key, a name or order ids (§7); a per-lookup audit row with a keyed HMAC of `phone_key` is **TK-Q10**. The hub request log redacts the `phone` query parameter (00 HPA v1.13, §4.3); this query ships only with that change |
| Errors | `VALIDATION_FAILED`, `PERMISSION_DENIED` |
| Audiences | none (a query; no frame carries `phone_key`) |

Privacy: owner Q1 (§12.3, 2026-11-07) kept the full phone in history and allowed a logged one-order lookup (`order-contact`); **R1 (2026-11-24) confirms matching orders by phone** for repeat and the customer's name (§7 Privacy).

### 6.18c `GET /queries/top-items`

⚠️ Added by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R9, G-15, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT). "Most ordered this hour" pinned in rush mode (TK-S01 HomeRush). Rush mode itself is UI only (§8); its trigger setting `rush_open_orders` is 01a v2.11's.

| | |
|---|---|
| Query | `{ minutes?: int 1–240 (default 60), order_type?: 'dine_in' \| 'takeaway' \| 'parking' }` (absent = every type); else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-9, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): the query string carries strings, so `minutes` is `z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(240)` and `order_type` a `z.enum` |
| Permission / scope | none: any staff session (`sessionKind: 'device'`); **`countsAsActivity: false`** (polled by the screen) |
| Counts | over orders with `placed_at ≥ now − minutes × 60 000` (hub clock), not cancelled, of the type: **`item` lines only** — no `deal` headers, no `deal_part` lines (a naan inside a deal is not a naan tile tap), no `option_child` lines (added through their parent's sheet), no return lines (`return_of` set); `units` = Σ live units (`units − removed_units − voided_units`; comped units still count; a weighed line counts 1); split parts (`part_of`) are separate quantity-1 lines and so add up; grouped by `item_id` (a variant is its own item, 01c V2) |
| Result | `{ items: [{ item_id, units }] }`, at most **12**, `units > 0`, by `units` desc then `item_id`; ids no longer in the config are returned as they are and skipped by the client. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-9, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): **a deal is never "top"** (headers and parts are left out), an owner-visible rule (TK-Q14) |
| Statements | **1** (`orders JOIN order_lines … GROUP BY l.item_id ORDER BY … LIMIT 12`; index `orders_placed`, then `order_lines_order`) |
| Errors | `VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Audiences | none |

### 6.19 Events (`schema_version` 1)
⚠️ Changed by run 3 verify (R3-M-8) On BusinessDayClosed, for each takeaway group whose order acquires day_closed_at in this fold, update its root cutoff in the same transaction using the event’s recorded_at. Set cutoff to recorded_at + 7,200,000 ms only if (a) the prior cutoff is null or later than recorded_at and (b) recorded_at precedes the 36-hour business-date ceiling. Skip carried orders that do not close. OrdersUnbilled may clear day_closed_at but preserves this cutoff. Additions share the original root and cannot reset it. Replay uses event order and recorded time, including across reopen and subsequent closes.

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): **`UnpaidHandoverApproved`** (order stream, stream ID = `order_id`, strict v1 `{ order_id, alert_id, approved_by_staff_id, due_minor }`; positive integer due). Define and register `packages/events/src/events/order/UnpaidHandoverApproved.v1.ts`. Fold only the named order: `unpaid_handover_approved_at = recorded_at` and `unpaid_handover_approved_due_minor = due_minor`. Cloud ingest stores the event; hub folds it and audit maps `unpaid_handover_approved` (TK-16). Audience = staff sockets and cashier kiosks, with `ORDER_EVENT_TYPES` and client `ORDER_FRAMES` (§8). Test 28 covers replay.

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-3) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): **`OpenOrderBillChanged`** (strict v1, order stream of the first affected order ID, `{ order_ids: string[], approval_cleared_order_ids: string[] }`). Both lists are unique, `order_ids` is nonempty, and clears are a subset. Register in `@pos/events`. No amounts or contact are broadcast. The fold clears both approval columns for the recorded subset. Register staff/kiosk delivery like `OrdersBilled`, excluding station/display, and add both frame lists. `OrdersBilled` and `OrdersUnbilled` also clear the two columns for their named orders; the empty-close appender records the clear in `OpenOrderBillChanged`.

⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-2) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-5) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8): The owning transaction appends one batched `OpenOrderBillChanged` after monetary changes fold, before commit (including payment add/remove/refund, split plan or close/reopen, discount or service charge, comp or reversal, line weight/removal/void/addition, and a config write that changes a projected bill). Use the same bill-key due for each eligible order on that live bill, with independent approval for each order. Resolve affected takeaway/parking orders once and recompute their keys once using §4.1, reusing available reads. Clear effective approval if the last bill closes or a reopen occurs, or if due now exceeds approved amount and the order has live unserved lines. Append the decided clear IDs for replay, with no billing reads in the orders fold. For an open request whose amount changed, close `obsolete` with note `due_changed` in that same transaction, unless the transaction's only money changes on that key are payments (added or removed) or a split close: then the request stays open, and the bill's last close closes it `bill_closed` (§3.7a (c)). Any other money change on the key closes it. A payment or split close still appends `OpenOrderBillChanged` and still clears an effective approval when the new due exceeds the approved amount. ⚠️ Changed by build (unpaid-3, owner ruling 2026-11-27: a payment alone does not close the request) Cancel/hand-over/last-close/day-close reasons in §3.7a take precedence. Budget up to 1 affected-order read, 1 key balance read and 1 open-request read across all affected keys, plus 01d’s batched close/result bound when needed. No reads per order.

| Event | Stream | Payload | Fold |
|---|---|---|---|
| `OrderPlaced` | order | 03a §4.2 + E-1 (no contact fields; `has_phone`) (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): `pickup_at_ms` now set for a takeaway with a pickup, §3.2 step 2b; the field already exists, no schema change) | `orders` row (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): + `pickup_at_ms`) |
| `OrderFoodServed` | order | `{ order_id }` (§3.3, after every `ItemServed` serving at least one food line) | — (02a folds it, P-1d) |

| `OrderContactSet` | order | `{ order_id, customer_name \| null, phone \| null }` (O17) | contact columns (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): + `phone_key = phoneKey(phone)`, §3.1; no event change) |

| `OrderLinesAdded` | order | 03a (returns only); always with `order_header` (03a v1.2 §4.2; ⚠️ Changed by owner ruling 2026-11-09 (E-4, 02b v1.0.2)) | — |
| `ItemRemoved` | order | `{ order_item_id, units? }` (Q-4) | ledger §3.4; zone count + (zone line) |
| `ItemVoided` | order | 03a + `refund_minor`, `comp_voided_minor` (04, §3.4) | ledger |
| `ItemServed` | order | `{ order_item_ids, source: 'tap' \| 'estimated' \| 'zone', served_at? }` (Q-6) | `served_at` (= `served_at` ?? `recorded_at`), `served_source` |
| `ItemReturned` | order | 03a | `returned_units` (engine view; money unchanged, O20) |
| `ItemsReleased`, `OrderBoosted` | order | 03a | `OrderBoosted` sets `pickup_boosted` on a pickup order (§3.8) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1) |
| `OrderCancelled` | order | `{ order_id, alert_id?: string }` (⚠️ Changed by 04 (row Q-1): optional `alert_id`, set when a void approval empties the order; the schema stays `.strict()` with the field declared; no version bump, R29) | `cancelled_at` (ignores `alert_id`) |
| `OrdersBilled` | order (first order_id) | `{ order_ids, bill_id }` (05) | `billed_at`; clear both effective approval columns ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8) |
| `OrdersUnbilled` ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-2) | order (first order_id) | `{ order_ids: string[] (≥ 1), bill_id, alert_id?: string }` (05; `.strict()`; `alert_id` present on an approval); joins `ORDER_EVENT_TYPES` and the client `ORDER_FRAMES` | `billed_at = NULL`, `day_closed_at = NULL`; clear both effective approval columns (§3.3) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-8) |
| `LineWeightSet` | order | `{ order_item_id, quantity, line_total_minor }` | line |
| `LineComped` | order | `{ order_item_id, units, value_minor, reason_code, note \| null, requester_staff_id, alert_id \| null, approver_staff_id \| null }` (children cascade as separate `LineComped`s in the same batch) | ledger |
| `LineCompRemoved` ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-4) | order | `{ order_item_id, units, comp_minor, reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }` (05; §3.9) | ledger: `comped_units −= units`, `comp_minor −= comp_minor` (never below 0) |
| `ZoneStockAdded` | table / `'zone:' + zone_id` | `{ zone_id, item_id, quantity, transfers: [{ stock_item_id, quantity }] }` | + |

**Audiences** (no catch-all on `order`, 01d To 02). Each audience function resolves `socket.staffId` through `StaffDirectory.resolve` (in memory) when it needs a grant. `OrderPlaced` → staff sockets and cashier kiosks (carries no contact). `OrderContactSet` → cashier kiosks and the placer's sockets only (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R6, owner 2026-11-11): the placer = the envelope `actor_id` of the order's `OrderPlaced`; was: not defined) (O17; 00's publisher sends one body per event, so the restriction is the audience, not field stripping). Zone events use stream id `'zone:' + zone_id`; 02a's fold and every `table`-stream reader select by event type, so zone ids never reach a table reader. Every other `order` event 02b's screens show — `ItemAdded`, `OrderLinesAdded`, `ItemRemoved`, `ItemVoided`, `ItemServed`, `ItemReturned`, `ItemsReleased`, `OrderBoosted`, `OrderCancelled`, `OrderFoodServed`, `OrderFoodCleared`, `OrdersBilled`, ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-2): `OrdersUnbilled`, `LineWeightSet`, ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-4): `UnpaidHandoverApproved` → staff sockets and cashier kiosks (menu prices are not secret; one body per event, run 3 #14). `LineComped` → cashier kiosks and sockets whose staff hold `apply-comp` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-39 R7, owner 2026-11-11): a `requires_approval` grant counts as holding it; was: not stated). `ZoneStock*` → staff sockets and cashier kiosks (counts are not secret; every order taker's tile shows them, run 4 M-3). KDS gets 03a / 03b station frames only. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-6): 02b also registers **`ItemStarted`, `ItemDone`, `ItemDoneUndone`, `FireDispatched` → staff sockets and cashier kiosks** in `orders/audiences.ts` (audiences are OR-ed, so 03b's station / point sets stay), so open order views refetch on stage changes (§8) (was: station frames only). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-10): **`SlipWarned` → staff sockets and cashier kiosks** (was: no audience).

**Size:** 10 commands + 3 kinds + 5 queries + 1 catch-up = **19** (the estimate tick is added with 03b). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-8): + `order-view` → 6 queries, **20** (inside the README budget of 10–25). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK audit, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): + `customer-orders`, `top-items` → 8 queries, **22** (inside 10–25). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 hostile run 2 (R2-M-6): + `RequestUnpaidHandover`, kind `hand_over_unpaid` → 11 commands + 4 kinds + 8 queries + 1 catch-up = **24** (inside 10–25).

---

## §7. Cross-cutting

| Item | Decision | Justification |
|---|---|---|
| Performance | `SubmitOrder` p99 < 60 ms (12 lines, peak engine); `eta-preview` < 40 ms; `seating-orders` < 30 ms | E2-ETA AC1 (≤ 1 s round trip on a cheap tablet) |
| Idempotency | D32; device ids; frozen cart | power cuts mid-rush (E5) |
| Races | single writer; ids, zone claims (per payload), token, stock, ledger inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; CHECKs as a backstop | last Paye, last raita, double comp |
| Queue security | client- and hub-side queueable list; session idle clock paused only while the device is disconnected, cap 4 h; ended sessions never accepted (queue log, "Send again"); device idle lock on shared tablets; residual in §3.12 | shared tablets, hub restore, Wi-Fi drops (§3.12) |
| Logs | `order_id`, `command_type`, `order_type`, line count, `queued`; never names, phones, totals (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): `customer-orders` logs `info { staff_id }` only, and the request log redacts the `phone` query parameter, 00 HPA v1.13; `pickup_at_ms` may be logged) | privacy, disputes |
| Privacy | contact only in `OrderContactSet` (audience: kiosks, placer), the per-socket catch-up and `order-contact` (permission `view-customer-contact`, not waiters by default; logged); it remains in the immutable log and the cloud (§12.3 Q1). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): **R1 (owner 2026-11-24) confirms matching orders by phone**: `customer-orders` (§6.18b, same permission, own orders only below `branch` scope, logged without values, phone redacted from the request log) returns a name, a visit count and the last 3 visits' items, never the phone; `phone_key` stays on the hub; `open-orders` carries no contact at all (R3); shared screens follow §8 (board tokens only, held drafts masked, counter display without contact). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-5, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT): **residual, stated for the owner (TK-Q13):** the catch-up part `orders` still delivers every open order's name and phone digits to a **cashier-kiosk device** whoever is signed in (O17 is per device), and v1.0.25 widens that set (paid takeaways stay until handed over). The kiosk's queue search runs on that copy (R3). The client **masks** names and digits in search results for a person without `view-customer-contact` (search still matches, §8), but the mask is a UI rule: the data is on the device. Accepted under R3 unless the owner rules otherwise | customer phone numbers |
| Boot | `orders` replays from checkpoint (00 M48) | no state outside events |

---

## §8. Client contract (`apps/client`, 01d's `/staff/` entry, ui-kit only)

- **Ordering screen** (inventory 02-S05): category rail, search, tiles (Sold out / Not now · hours / "4 left" / clock "25 min" when `slow_item_threshold_ms` is passed / "Only 2 left" caps the stepper, O15); cart sorted by ETA (E2-ETA AC2) with each group's promise as a clock time; options sheet with defaults visible and linked-option availability; kitchen note ≤ 80, order note ≤ 200; Hold (device draft); Send (freezes the cart). A takeaway / parking `OrderView` opened from a deep link takes the `order-view` body the tap already read, and makes no second read for its first render.

- **Queue:** "Reconnecting… 2 orders waiting to send" (B02); on reconnect the queue drains first, as the same person, with no new login; queue log ("Send again" as the person now logged in, "Discard", "Copy to a new order"); Day not started → "Start day" for `OpenBusinessDay` holders, "Ask the manager to start the day" for others. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-2): "as the same person" holds within the page's life only; after a reload / restart every entry is in the queue log as "Send again as {current person}" (§3.12). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-20): "Start day" (shown to people with `manage-stock` at `branch`) opens `/admin/stock/` (the admin page's Start day, admin PIN login), since `OpenBusinessDay` is an admin-session command; others see "Ask the manager to start the day" (was: "for `OpenBusinessDay` holders", sent from the staff screen).
- **Device idle lock** (00 H-1, simplified): the app runs its own idle timer of the session's idle TTL (`SessionStarted.idle_ttl_ms`, kept with the session at login; H-1 adds it to the login response if absent) on taps, connected or not, **comparing wall-clock time since the last tap on app resume / `visibilitychange` and before acting on any tap** (a sleeping WebView suspends timers, run 4 m-9). At expiry it shows the lock screen, drains the queue first, then sends `POST /auth/logout` (offline: a **pending logout** kept by the app, not a queue entry, sent before the next login) and offers login. ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-21): `idle_ttl_ms ≤ 0` = lock at once (as the hub treats it). ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-2): the pending logout is kept in memory with the token; after a restart it is dropped (the hub idles the session out, 4 h cap) (was: kept until the next login).

  - ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-B-2): **Counter-facing display.** A second kiosk browser window receives cart lines, total and Ready by through `BroadcastChannel`. Customer name and phone stay off this display. Pilot card payment is on the counter machine with typed reference (D4).
  - ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4): **Pass “Handed to customer” (OR-2).** Requires both `run-pass` and `mark-served`. Follow the shared gate above on the card’s order. Apply whole-order readiness including immediate lines still to make, with no-station lines held only until the effective pickup. An incomplete bag clears the counter alert and amber clock until complete again (D8). Keep 03b’s connected check, checked-send queue and undo.
  - **Hand-over undo (5 s):** after Hand over the client waits 5 s before sending `MarkServed` (toast "48 handed over · Undo"); Undo cancels it unsent. The pending `MarkServed` is sent at once on `visibilitychange` to hidden and on `pagehide` / unload, and goes through the device queue like any `MarkServed` (§3.12), so a closed tab retains the pending send; a command-time refusal goes to the queue log and the board updates only on the real served frame. ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4): Queued customer hand-over keeps the flag and must pass §6.4 at execution.
  - **Phone and pickup (R1, R4):** phone optional; a typed phone must be valid (fix or clear it). `customer-orders` (holders of `view-customer-contact`, online only) shows "Usman · 6 orders" and Repeat (lines with `on_menu: false` skipped, "2 items no longer on the menu"). Pickup chips Now / +15 / +30 / +45 / Time… send `pickup_at_ms`; the picker's range is the hub's (earliest = now + 10 min, latest = end of day, §3.2 step 2b); a `422 PICKUP_TIME_INVALID` shows its `earliest_ms` / `latest_ms`. An addition to a token pre-fills the root's pickup only while it is ≥ hub now + 10 min (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (m-8, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)); the picker's range is `eta-preview`'s `pickup_bounds`; a `422 too_early` at Send re-previews and shows the new range; `no_time_left` offers "Now" or "Close the day first" (M-3). Day not started: `eta-preview`'s `409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN` shows "Times show after Start day" and blocks before the first item (§6.14).
  - **Tile wait estimate (audit; 03a To-02 #9):** a tile's wait = `stations-live` `wait_ms` + prep + cook (+ takeaway padding) — an **estimate**; the cart's `eta-preview` stays the promise. On a takeaway tile there is **no zone "Now"** (zone stock never serves takeaway, `build-submit.ts:73`, §3.10). A stock-served (pre-cooked) item shows its **reheat time** (`stock_time_ms`); "Now" only for a ready item or `stock_time_ms = 0`.
- **Rules:** buttons from effective permissions (R5); Urdu / English, Latin digits (R20); no service worker except the flagged kiosk; no secure-context APIs; ULIDs from `crypto.getRandomValues` (R14, R15); Capacitor LAN transport and token storage per 9b (README :163).

## §9. Error catalogue additions (Foundation §6.2, `@pos/domain/src/errors.ts`)

| Code | Status | `details` | When |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ORDER_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ order_id } \| { order_item_id }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-28, AD-33, owner 2026-11-11): `{ order_item_id }` for a command keyed only by line — `SetLineWeight`, `CompLine`, `ReturnToKitchen`; was: `{ order_id }`) | unknown order or line |
| `ORDER_ITEM_ID_IN_USE` | 409 | `{ order_item_id } \| { order_id }` | id already in use (O3) |
| `ITEM_UNAVAILABLE` | 409 | `{ item_id \| deal_id }` | manual Off, or a required group unsatisfiable |
| `ITEM_OUT_OF_HOURS` | 409 | `{ item_id \| deal_id, opens_at_ms \| null }` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.4 (AD-19, owner 2026-11-11): `deal_id` for a deal outside its windows; was: `{ item_id, opens_at_ms \| null }`) | outside a window (S47) |
| `ITEM_SOLD_OUT` | 409 | `{ item_id, available }` | `sold_out` line short of stock |
| `OPTION_CHOICE_INVALID` | 422 | `{ order_item_id, violations }` | `checkOptionChoices` |
| `ZONE_ITEM_NOT_CONFIGURED` | 422 | `{ zone_id, item_id }` | refill / count of a non-zone item |
| `STOCK_INSUFFICIENT` | 409 | `{ stock_item_id, available }` | refill transfer short |
| `LINE_NOT_WEIGHED` | 409 | `{ order_item_id }` | weight on a non-weighed line |
| `LINE_WEIGHT_PENDING` | 409 | `{ order_item_id }` | comp on a pending weight |
| `LINE_ALREADY_SETTLED` | 409 | `{ order_item_id }` | weight change after a unit left billing |
| `COMP_NOT_ALLOWED` | 409 | `{ reason: 'not_live' \| 'return_line' \| 'deal_part' \| 'zone_line' }` | §3.9 |
| `COMP_PENDING` | 409 | `{ alert_id }` | an open comp request on the line |
| `ORDER_BILLED` | 409 | `{ order_id }` | edit after the bill closed |
| `PICKUP_TIME_INVALID` (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R4, G-2, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT); ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-3, m-7, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT)) | 422 | `{ reason: 'no_time_left' \| 'other_day' \| 'too_early' \| 'after_day_end', pickup_at_ms, earliest_ms, latest_ms }` | a takeaway `pickup_at_ms` when no time is left in the open day (`earliest > latest`), before the open day's start, before hub now (or the accepted `queued_at`) + 10 min, or after the end of the open business day (§3.2 step 2b); `SubmitOrder` and `eta-preview` (was: `too_early` \| `after_day_end` only) |

Reused: `BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, `ORDER_NOT_IN_ENGINE`, `RETURN_ALREADY_OPEN`, `STATION_PAUSED`, `ITEM_WRONG_STAGE`, `MENU_ITEM_NOT_FOUND`, `DEAL_NOT_FOUND`, `ZONE_NOT_FOUND`, `TABLE_*` (02a), `SESSION_EXPIRED`, `PERMISSION_DENIED`, `VALIDATION_FAILED`.

A later due increase or permission loss before queued/live execution refuses atomically with no served event; both checks read fresh state. |

Approved void appends the close outside apply. Paid/empty/day close reuse the open-list read and still count the 01d close/result. A carried Due order’s open request closes `day_closed` and is absent from next day’s requests; each close sends the requester `request_result`. |

A duplicate ask while a same-amount request is pending reuses its ID. Approve, pay without collecting, reopen with positive due → false. Raise due above the stored approved amount before hand-over, then lower it → still false; replay matches. Confirm each sibling needs its own approval. |

10. `SetLineWeight` (21).

17. Queries §6.15–6.18, catch-up, audiences (32, 33).

21. Client: table view orders part, comp card, zone stock, open orders, contact.
22. Perf (34); acceptance pass; README rows closed.

## §12. Definition of Done, checklist, questions, deferred, reviews

### 12.1 Definition of Done

**Approval steps:** the developer approves v1.0; the §4.5 rows are applied in their own spec bumps (F v1.11, 00 v1.8, 01a v2.3, 01b v1.7, 01c v1.1.2, 02a v1.1; 03a v1.2 together with the engine lane's as-built, G1-1); README index and carried rows updated in the same change. Then the plan (`docs/plans/02b-plan.md`). All §10 tests green under the gate; §4.5 rows approved and applied first (F v1.11, 00 v1.8, 01a v2.3, 01b v1.7, 01c v1.1.2, 02a v1.1, 03a v1.2); every command cites its section; statement counts asserted; README index and carried rows updated.

### 12.3 Open questions

| # | Question | Default until answered |
|---|---|---|
| Q1 | (owner) A customer's phone and name are kept in the order history for ever and copied to the cloud (in `OrderContactSet`); staff with `view-customer-contact` (not waiters by default) can look one up, logged. OK, or should 02b keep only a masked number in history? | **Answered (owner, 2026-11-07): keep.** Full phone and name stay in history and the cloud. |
| Q4 | Wi-Fi queue rulings | **Answered and simplified (owner, 2026-11-07):** the login does not expire while the tablet is off the Wi-Fi (up to 4 h), so the queue goes out as soon as the Wi-Fi is back, with no wait; the tablet locks itself after the idle time; anything that still fails waits in the queue log for one tap "Send again". |
| Q3 | Proxy rulings of run 2 | **Answered (owner, 2026-11-07):** remade dish stays billed unless comped (O20) — yes; voided comp reads as a voided comp (O21) — yes; joined tables show **one** late card (O18, a screen rule since the simplification); table state for zone / ready items — open, Q5. |
| Q5 | Ready items and table state | **Answered (owner, 2026-11-07):** (a) water bottles and similar sit in the zone in winter, kept ready if someone asks — zone stock as built; a ready item not in the zone goes to its station / counter like any line; (b) zone refill is a permission (`manage-zone-stock`) given to whoever does it — manager, floor manager or a dedicated person — and the low / out alerts go to them; (c) items that are reheated from stock are pre-cooked stock items (food lines), not ready items. Table turns green only on food (§3.3). |
| Q2 | Can a comp be undone before the bill? | ~~no; 05 handles a wrong comp at the bill~~ ⚠️ Changed by 05 (row Q-4): undone only before billing, by 05's `RemoveComp` with approval (`LineCompRemoved`, §3.9) |
| TK-Q1 | ⚠️ Added by v1.0.25 (R4; DRAFT): the **earliest pickup**. R4 fixes only the latest. | Recommended default: hub now (or the accepted `queued_at`) + **10 min** (`PICKUP_MIN_LEAD_MS`, a constant) |
| TK-Q2 | **Accepted (owner 2026-11-24).** Business-day end | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): 01a’s `business_day_end_local_ms` defaults to 04:00 local. Use `businessDayLatestMs` (§3.2 step 2b, TK-8); `no_time_left` when earliest exceeds latest. |
| TK-Q3 | Queued phone order arriving after its pickup | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-1): Accept within the day and schedule as soon as feasible. The engine, event and orders projection keep the original time (§3.2 step 2b). Refuse a pickup before the open day as `other_day`. |
| TK-Q4 | Hand-off G-3 (name + phone required with a pickup) vs R1. | Recommended default: optional at the hub (R1 wins); the client prompts, never blocks |
| TK-Q5 | Mobile typed without its `0`. | Recommended default adopted (run 1 m-2): `3` + 9 digits → `92` + d (rule 3b); confirm |
| TK-Q6 | `ready_at` without a Pass point. | Resolved by run 1 M-2: the line rule also uses the engine's `done_at` and a no-station line's `added_at`, so a kitchen with no Pass still gets a ready time |
| TK-Q7 | Paid, not handed-over takeaway at day close. | ⚠️ Resolved by owner ruling DC-2 (2026-11-27): it **stays open** across the close (shown next day with its date) and leaves when handed over, cancelled or abandoned (§3.3 Day close). Was: "leaves at the close (day close unchanged)" |
| TK-Q8 | (run 1 B-1) `bill.due_minor` needs 05's `keyBalances`. | Resolved: 05 v1.0.35 §6.2a carries it (row Q-8, 1 statement); 05's test #83 change remains for 05 (row TK-5) |
| TK-Q9 | `?phone=` in the GET query string. | Recommended default: the request log redacts `phone` (00 HPA v1.13, row TK-6); `customer-orders` ships only with it |
| TK-Q10 | (run 1 m-3) Audit of phone lookups. | Recommended default: log `{ staff_id, matched }` only; option: a per-lookup audit row with a keyed HMAC of `phone_key` (no digits) |
| TK-Q11 | (run 1 m-12) Held-draft name mask. | Recommended default: "Customer" (full mask, as R3 says "mask") |
| TK-Q12 | **ANSWERED (owner 2026-11-24, OR-2)** — was BLOCKER-for-approval (run 1 M-2). Does a Pass `Handover` or the estimate tick count as handed to the customer for a takeaway? | **No** for a Pass→staff `Handover` and the estimate tick (§3.3, §3.7). Handed to the customer = a `MarkServed` of the lines, from the counter **or from the Pass's "Handed to customer"** (03b v1.0.16 §3.4, owner OR-2) |
| TK-Q13 | **Accepted (owner 2026-11-24).** Kiosk search | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): Search matches catch-up contact on the kiosk. Mask displayed name/digits without `view-customer-contact` (§7–8). |
| TK-Q14 | (run 1 m-9) Deals in `top-items`. | Recommended default: never "top" (items only) |
| TK-Q15 | (run 1 M-1 c) Token slip field on `SubmitOrder`. | Resolved by 03c v1.0.16 rows Q-TK-1–Q-TK-4 (carried exactly; 03c's owner decision D-1) |
| TK-Q16 | (run 1 m-1) `orders_open_takeaway_paid` scans the day's paid takeaways. | Recommended default: keep the `EXISTS`, measure with `test:perf` (test 83); `handed_at` column only if it fails |
| TK-Q17 | **Accepted (owner 2026-11-24, 00 OD-10).** Order ID | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): Require `ULID_RE` (§3.2 step 3, test 84). Before it lands `statusLink` returns null for non-ULIDs. |
| TK-Q18 | **Accepted (short list #5).** Parking | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): OR-7 includes parking with nullable token and `PARKING` identifier. |
| TK-Q19 | **Accepted (short list #5, D2).** Partial hand-over | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-M-4) ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): Allowed only if every touched Due, unapproved order retains a live unserved line (§3.7a). |
| TK-Q20 | **Accepted (short list #5).** Requester | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): The requester cannot approve. A lone holder sees “Waiting — find a manager.” |
| TK-Q21 | **Accepted (short list #5).** Request family | ⚠️ Changed by run 3 (R3-m-1): `urgent`. |
| Q6 | ⚠️ Added by v1.0.11 (owner ruling 2026-11-12, Q-8): the ruling sets `order-view`'s scope "as `MarkServed`" for **takeaway / parking** orders only. Who may read a **dine-in** order through it? | **Answered (owner 2026-11-12, Q-27 = Rec):** as `seating-orders` (any staff session, 0 statements, so the bound stays 2 + 1); the dine-in table view keeps using `seating-orders`. |

### 12.4 Deferred until needed

Delivery and web types; later child additions ("one more naan" attached) with README :201's rule; ~~reorder~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R1, G-4, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): un-deferred — repeat by phone, `customer-orders` §6.18b); kitchen-note edit after submit; manager view of held orders; sibling-zone transfer (Z6), day-end zone recount prompt (Z8), nested zones and floor routes; estimated Served (with 03b); hub-down manager alert (06 / 01e); ~~customer tracking (P2)~~ (⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 (02b-TK R5, owner 2026-11-24; DRAFT): un-deferred — the customer status page from the slip QR is in the pilot: a public LAN route with a guest-network exemption, an HMAC-signed link and an in-memory rate limiter, specified in **00 Hub Platform Access v1.13** (and Foundation D28); the read it serves is 02b's export **`takeawayProgress`** (§4.2; ⚠️ Changed by v1.0.25 run 1 (M-1 (a), m-11, `docs/reviews/02b_v1.0.25_hostile_run1.md`; DRAFT); was: "comes with that bump").

### 12.5 Hostile self-review (v0.3, kept; v0.4 additions in §12.7)

- **Money:** one ledger for comp, remove and void; CHECKs and a property test; 04 bound to it (To 04).
- **Engine reality:** Q-3, Q-4 stated against the code on `module-03a` (`apply-event.ts`, `stock.ts`) with their cost; `planOrder` / `calculateETA` with `from_stock`.
- **Queue:** queueable list, paused idle clock with a cap, device idle lock and no ended-session acceptance close the shared-tablet path (§3.12).
- **Replay:** the projection reads only its own events.
- **Known dependency risks:** §11 steps 7, 9, 12, 15, 18 wait on 03a v1.2, 01a v2.3, 00 v1.8, 02a v1.1; the Android queue needs 9b's app.
- **YAGNI:** no copies nobody reads, no `preview_seq`, no tick before 03b, no return note.
